called by the API endpoint routers/workflow.py. So staging-gate-smoke (scripts/prod_smoke_test.py),

    72	#
    73	# Pipeline of sources, first non-empty wins:
    74	#   1. The request's explicit `target_platform` field.

    76	#   3. Default — `host_native` profile (T1-A default).
    77	#
    78	# Returns the parsed profile dict or None if none can be loaded; the
    79	# validator's `target_profile=None` path preserves pre-64-C behaviour.
    80	
    81	def _resolve_target_profile(explicit: str | None) -> dict | None:
    82	    from pathlib import Path
    83	    import yaml as _yaml
    84	    from backend.sdk_provisioner import _validate_platform_name, _platform_profile
    85	
    86	    # 1. Explicit request field.
    87	    if explicit:
    88	        name = explicit.strip()
    89	    else:
    90	        name = ""
    91	
    92	    # 2. Fall back to hardware_manifest.
    93	    if not name:
    94	        manifest = Path("configs") / "hardware_manifest.yaml"
    95	        if manifest.is_file():
    96	            try:
    97	                m = _yaml.safe_load(manifest.read_text()) or {}
    98	                name = (
    99	                    (m.get("project") or {}).get("target_platform")
   100	                    or m.get("target_platform")
   101	                    or ""
   102	                ).strip()
   103	            except Exception as exc:
   104	                logger.debug("hardware_manifest parse failed: %s", exc)
   105	
   106	    # 3. Final default matches T1-A.
   107	    if not name:
   108	        name = "host_native"
   109	
   110	    if not _validate_platform_name(name):
   111	        logger.debug("target_platform %r rejected by validator", name)
   112	        return None
   113	    profile_path = _platform_profile(name)
   114	    if profile_path is None or not profile_path.exists():
   115	        logger.debug("target_platform %r: no profile YAML", name)
   116	        return None
   117	    try:
   118	        return _yaml.safe_load(profile_path.read_text()) or {}
   119	    except Exception as exc:
   120	        logger.debug("profile %s parse failed: %s", name, exc)
   121	        return None

   148	
   149	def _prewarm_enabled() -> bool:
   150	    """Pre-warm is opt-in. Default OFF because v1 cannot yet mount the
   151	    per-agent workspace into the pre-warmed container (that requires
   152	    agent→task assignment which happens LATER). Operators that run a
   153	    shared workspace layout can turn this on experimentally.
   154	
   155	    M5: even with OMNISIGHT_PREWARM_ENABLED=true, ``prewarm_policy``
   156	    can short-circuit ("disabled") — skip the background hook entirely
   157	    in that case to keep metrics clean and save a task schedule.
   158	    """
   159	    import os as _os
   160	    raw = (_os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_PREWARM_ENABLED") or "false").strip().lower()
   161	    if raw not in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}:
   162	        return False
   163	    try:
   164	        from backend import sandbox_prewarm as _pw
   165	        if _pw.get_policy() == "disabled":
   166	            return False
   167	    except Exception:
   168	        pass
   169	    return True

   172	async def _prewarm_in_background(dag: DAG) -> None:
   173	    """Fire-and-forget hook. Always swallows exceptions — pre-warm
   174	    failure must not surface to the DAG submit caller.
   175	
   176	    M5: resolve the current tenant from the request context so the
   177	    speculative container lands in the right per-tenant bucket.
   178	    """
   179	    try:
   180	        from pathlib import Path
   181	        from backend.workspace import _WORKSPACES_ROOT  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
   182	        from backend import sandbox_prewarm as _pw
   183	        from backend.db_context import current_tenant_id
   184	        tid = current_tenant_id()
   185	        shared = Path(_WORKSPACES_ROOT) / "_prewarm"
   186	        shared.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   187	        await _pw.prewarm_for(dag, shared, tenant_id=tid)
   188	    except Exception as exc:
   189	        logger.debug("prewarm hook swallowed error: %s", exc)
   190	
   191	
   192	async def _cancel_prewarm(reason: str) -> None:
   193	    """Mutation / abort path — drop stale pre-warms so their lifetime
   194	    budget isn't burned waiting for a task that'll never consume.
   195	
   196	    M5: scoped to the current tenant — cancelling another tenant's
   197	    speculation on our mutation would be both wrong and a covert DoS
   198	    vector.
   199	    """
   200	    try:
   201	        from backend import sandbox_prewarm as _pw
   202	        from backend.db_context import current_tenant_id
   203	        tid = current_tenant_id()
   204	        n = await _pw.cancel_all(reason=reason, tenant_id=tid)
   205	        if n:
   206	            logger.info("prewarm: cancelled %d container(s) on %s", n, reason)
   207	    except Exception as exc:
   208	        logger.debug("prewarm cancel swallowed error: %s", exc)

   214	#
   215	# Dry-run validation for the authoring UI. Pure function: parses the
   216	# payload against the Pydantic schema, then runs the 7-rule validator.
   217	# Nothing is persisted and no workflow_run is created — the editor's

   236	@router.post("/validate")
   237	async def validate_dag(req: DAGValidateRequest,
   238	                       _user=Depends(_au.require_operator)) -> dict:
   239	    """Run validator without storing. Returns either
   240	    `{ok: true}` or `{ok: false, stage: schema|semantic, errors: [...]}`.
   241	    Each semantic error carries `rule`, `task_id` (or null), `message`.
   242	
   243	    Phase 64-C-LOCAL S4 additions in the response:
   244	      * `t3_runner` — "local" / "bundle" / ... — what the resolver
   245	        would pick for every t3 task in this DAG. Lets the editor UI
   246	        render per-node ⚡ (LOCAL) vs 🔗 (remote bundle) chips.
   247	      * `target_platform` — the profile name the resolver used, so the
   248	        UI can display it alongside the chip."""
   249	    # Stage 1: Pydantic schema (shape).
   250	    try:
   251	        dag = DAG.model_validate(req.dag)
   252	    except Exception as exc:
   253	        return {
   254	            "ok": False,
   255	            "stage": "schema",
   256	            "errors": [{"rule": "schema", "task_id": None, "message": str(exc)}],
   257	        }
   258	
   259	    # Resolve once — validator + UI-hint both want the same answer.
   260	    profile = _resolve_target_profile(req.target_platform)

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Phase 56 — durable workflow checkpointing.
     2	
     3	The smallest useful version of "the agent loop survives a crash":
     4	
     5	  - `workflow_runs`  one row per logical execution (invoke /
     6	    pipeline_phase / decision_chain / report-gen).
     7	  - `workflow_steps` append-only checkpoint log; (run_id,
     8	    idempotency_key) UNIQUE so repeating the same step returns the
     9	    cached output instead of re-running side effects.
    10	
    11	Usage:
    12	
    13	    run = await workflow.start("invoke", metadata={"user": "alice"})
    14	
    15	    @workflow.step(run, "fetch_repo")
    16	    async def fetch_repo():
    17	        ...
    18	        return {"sha": "abc"}
    19	
    20	    sha = (await fetch_repo())["sha"]
    21	
    22	    @workflow.step(run, f"compile/{sha}")
    23	    async def compile():
    24	        ...
    25	        return {"image": "fw.bin"}
    26	
    27	If the backend crashes after `fetch_repo` succeeded, on restart the
    28	caller invokes the same code path with the same run id — the
    29	decorator sees the cached step output and returns it without
    30	re-running. `compile` then runs for the first time.
    31	
    32	Resume helper: `await workflow.replay(run_id)` — returns the run +
    33	its step list so a higher level loop can decide how to continue.
    34	"""
    35	
    36	from __future__ import annotations
    37	
    38	import json
    39	import logging
    40	import time
    41	import uuid
    42	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    43	from typing import Any, Awaitable, Callable, Optional

    45	from backend.agent_guild_dual_write import guild_id_from_values, sync_agent_type_guild_id

    52	#  Dataclasses
    53	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
    54	
    55	RunStatus = str  # "running" | "completed" | "failed" | "halted"
    56	
    57	
    58	@dataclass
    59	class WorkflowRun:
    60	    id: str
    61	    kind: str
    62	    started_at: float
    63	    status: RunStatus = "running"
    64	    completed_at: Optional[float] = None
    65	    last_step_id: Optional[str] = None
    66	    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    67	    version: int = 0
    68	
    69	
    70	@dataclass
    71	class StepRecord:
    72	    id: str
    73	    run_id: str
    74	    idempotency_key: str
    75	    started_at: float
    76	    completed_at: Optional[float] = None
    77	    output: Any = None
    78	    error: Optional[str] = None
    79	
    80	    @property
    81	    def is_done(self) -> bool:
    82	        return self.completed_at is not None and self.error is None

    85	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

   100	def _uid(prefix: str) -> str:
   101	    return f"{prefix}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}"
   102

   104	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
   105	#  Public API
   106	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

   108	
   109	async def start(kind: str, *, metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   110	                run_id: str | None = None,
   111	                dag: Any | None = None,
   112	                parent_plan_id: int | None = None,
   113	                mutation_round: int = 0,
   114	                target_profile: dict | None = None) -> WorkflowRun:
   115	    """Open a new workflow run, or attach to an existing one if
   116	    `run_id` is supplied (used by resume paths).
   117	
   118	    Phase 56-DAG-B: when `dag` is supplied (a `dag_schema.DAG`), the
   119	    plan is persisted to `dag_plans`, validated by `dag_validator`,
   120	    and bidirectionally linked to the new workflow_run. The run is
   121	    only set to status='executing' if validation passes; failed
   122	    validation leaves the plan at status='failed' and the run still
   123	    starts (caller decides whether to mutate via Phase 56-DAG-C).
   124	
   125	    Phase 64-C-LOCAL S4: `target_profile` flows through to the
   126	    validator so t3 tasks with a host==target profile get the
   127	    tier-relaxation treatment (pre-64-C callers pass None and keep
   128	    the narrow hardware-bridge-only behaviour).
   129	
   130	    `parent_plan_id` + `mutation_round` chain mutation history.
   131	    """
   132	    if run_id:
   133	        existing = await get_run(run_id)
   134	        if existing:
   135	            return existing
   136	    run_metadata = sync_agent_type_guild_id(metadata)
   137	    run = WorkflowRun(
   138	        id=run_id or _uid("wf"),
   139	        kind=kind,
   140	        started_at=time.time(),
   141	        metadata=run_metadata,
   142	    )
   143	    async with get_pool().acquire() as conn:
   144	        await conn.execute(
   145	            "INSERT INTO workflow_runs "
   146	            "(id, kind, started_at, status, metadata, tenant_id, guild_id) "
   147	            "VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'running', $4, $5, $6)",
   148	            run.id, run.kind, run.started_at,
   149	            json.dumps(run.metadata), tenant_insert_value(),
   150	            guild_id_from_values(run.metadata),
   151	        )
   152	
   153	    # Q.3-SUB-1 (#297): cross-device SSE broadcast. Best-effort —
   154	    # the workflow row is already committed, a flaky bus must not
   155	    # fail start().
   156	    try:
   157	        from backend.events import emit_workflow_updated
   158	        emit_workflow_updated(run.id, run.status, run.version, kind=run.kind)
   159	    except Exception as exc:
   160	        logger.debug("emit_workflow_updated on start failed: %s", exc)
   161	
   162	    # Phase 56-DAG-B: persist + validate + link the plan.
   163	    if dag is not None:
   164	        try:
   165	            from backend import dag_storage as _ds
   166	            from backend import dag_validator as _dv
   167	            result = _dv.validate(dag, target_profile=target_profile)
   168	            plan = await _ds.save_plan(
   169	                dag, run_id=run.id,
   170	                parent_plan_id=parent_plan_id,
   171	                status="failed" if not result.ok else "validated",
   172	                mutation_round=mutation_round,
   173	                validation_errors=result.errors if not result.ok else None,
   174	            )
   175	            await _ds.attach_to_run(plan.id, run.id)
   176	            if result.ok:
   177	                await _ds.set_status(plan.id, "executing")
   178	        except Exception as exc:
   179	            # Plan attach failure must not break workflow.start.
   180	            logger.warning("dag plan attach failed for run=%s: %s", run.id, exc)
   181	
   182	    logger.info("workflow.start kind=%s id=%s%s",
   183	                kind, run.id,
   184	                f" dag={dag.dag_id}" if dag is not None else "")
   185	    return run
   186	
   187	
   188	async def mutate_workflow(old_run_id: str, new_dag: Any, *,
   189	                          mutation_round: int) -> WorkflowRun:
   190	    """Phase 56-DAG-B: open a successor workflow_run for a mutated DAG.
   191	
   192	    The old run is preserved (Phase 56's append-only invariant) and
   193	    marked with `successor_run_id` pointing at the new run. The new
   194	    plan inherits the old plan's id as `parent_plan_id` so the
   195	    mutation chain is replayable.
   196	    """
   197	    from backend import dag_storage as _ds
   198	    old_plan = await _ds.get_plan_by_run(old_run_id)
   199	    parent_plan_id = old_plan.id if old_plan else None
   200	
   201	    new_run = await start(
   202	        kind="invoke",  # mutated runs reuse the invoke kind by default
   203	        dag=new_dag,
   204	        parent_plan_id=parent_plan_id,
   205	        mutation_round=mutation_round,
   206	        metadata={"mutated_from": old_run_id, "mutation_round": mutation_round},
   207	    )
   208	    await _ds.link_successor(old_run_id, new_run.id)
   209	    if old_plan and old_plan.status in {"validated", "executing", "failed"}:
   210	        try:
   211	            await _ds.set_status(old_plan.id, "mutated")
   212	        except Exception as exc:
   213	            logger.warning("mark mutated failed for plan=%s: %s",
   214	                           old_plan.id, exc)
   215	    return new_run
   216	
   217	
   218	def _row_to_run(r) -> "WorkflowRun":
   219	    return WorkflowRun(
   220	        id=r["id"], kind=r["kind"], started_at=r["started_at"],
   221	        completed_at=r["completed_at"], status=r["status"],
   222	        last_step_id=r["last_step_id"],
   223	        metadata=json.loads(r["metadata"] or "{}"),
   224	        version=r["version"],
   225	    )
   226	
   227	
   228	async def get_run(run_id: str) -> Optional[WorkflowRun]:
   229	    params: list = [run_id]
   230	    conditions = ["id = $1"]
   231	    tenant_where_pg(conditions, params)
   232	    sql = (
   233	        f"SELECT {_RUN_COLS} FROM workflow_runs "
   234	        f"WHERE {' AND '.join(conditions)}"
   235	    )
   236	    async with get_pool().acquire() as conn:
   237	        row = await conn.fetchrow(sql, *params)
   238	    return _row_to_run(row) if row else None
   239	
   240	
   241	async def list_runs(
   242	    status: str | None = None, limit: int = 50,
   243	) -> list[WorkflowRun]:
   244	    conditions: list[str] = []
   245	    p: list = []
   246	    tenant_where_pg(conditions, p)
   247	    if status:
   248	        p.append(status)
   249	        conditions.append(f"status = ${len(p)}")
   250	    sql = f"SELECT {_RUN_COLS} FROM workflow_runs"
   251	    if conditions:
   252	        sql += " WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions)
   253	    p.append(limit)
   254	    sql += f" ORDER BY started_at DESC LIMIT ${len(p)}"
   255	    async with get_pool().acquire() as conn:
   256	        rows = await conn.fetch(sql, *p)
   257	    return [_row_to_run(r) for r in rows]

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Phase 56-DAG-B — DAG plan persistence.
     2	
     3	Thin wrapper over the `dag_plans` table introduced in Phase 56-DAG-B.
     4	Validation lives in `dag_validator.py` (Phase 56-DAG-A); this module
     5	only persists / queries / chains plans.
     6	
     7	Status state-machine (no skips, no reverse transitions):
     8	
     9	       (DAG submitted)
    10	              │
    11	              ▼
    12	          pending ──────► validated ──────► executing ──────► completed
    13	              │                                  │
    14	              ▼                                  ▼
    15	           failed                            mutated  ──► (new pending plan)
    16	                                                 │
    17	                                                 ▼
    18	                                            exhausted
    19	
    20	Phase-3-Runtime-v2 SP-5.1 (2026-04-21): ported from aiosqlite compat
    21	wrapper to native asyncpg pool. ``set_status`` and ``attach_to_run``
    22	run inside ``async with conn.transaction()`` so their multi-statement
    23	read-then-write sequences don't interleave with concurrent writers.
    24	
    25	Module-global audit (SOP Step 1, 2026-04-21 rule): this module's only
    26	top-level state is ``logger``, the ``_VALID_STATUSES`` set, the
    27	``_ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS`` dict, and the ``StoredPlan`` dataclass — all
    28	stable constants derived at import time. Each worker computes the
    29	same values from the same source, so no cross-worker coordination
    30	concerns beyond what PG row locks already provide.
    31	"""
    32	
    33	from __future__ import annotations
    34	
    35	import json
    36	import logging
    37	import time
    38	from dataclasses import dataclass
    39	from typing import Optional
    40	
    41	from backend.dag_schema import DAG
    42	from backend.dag_validator import ValidationError as DagValidationError
    43	from backend.db_pool import get_pool
    44	
    45	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    46	
    47	
    48	_VALID_STATUSES = {
    49	    "pending", "validated", "failed",
    50	    "executing", "completed", "mutated", "exhausted",
    51	}
    52	
    53	# Forward transitions only — reject anything else at write time.
    54	_ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: dict[str, set[str]] = {
    55	    "pending":   {"validated", "failed"},
    56	    "validated": {"executing", "mutated", "exhausted"},
    57	    "failed":    {"mutated", "exhausted"},
    58	    "executing": {"completed", "mutated", "exhausted"},
    59	    "completed": set(),
    60	    "mutated":   set(),
    61	    "exhausted": set(),
    62	}
    63	
    64	
    65	@dataclass
    66	class StoredPlan:
    67	    id: int
    68	    dag_id: str
    69	    run_id: Optional[str]
    70	    parent_plan_id: Optional[int]
    71	    json_body: str
    72	    status: str
    73	    mutation_round: int
    74	    validation_errors: Optional[str]
    75	    created_at: float
    76	    updated_at: float
    77	
    78	    def dag(self) -> DAG:
    79	        """Re-hydrate the DAG model from JSON."""
    80	        return DAG.model_validate(json.loads(self.json_body))
    81	
    82	    def errors(self) -> list[dict]:
    83	        if not self.validation_errors:
    84	            return []
    85	        try:
    86	            return json.loads(self.validation_errors)
    87	        except Exception:
    88	            return []
    89

    91	def _row_to_plan(row) -> StoredPlan:
    92	    return StoredPlan(
    93	        id=row["id"], dag_id=row["dag_id"], run_id=row["run_id"],
    94	        parent_plan_id=row["parent_plan_id"], json_body=row["json_body"],
    95	        status=row["status"], mutation_round=row["mutation_round"],
    96	        validation_errors=row["validation_errors"],
    97	        created_at=row["created_at"], updated_at=row["updated_at"],
    98	    )
    99	
   100	
   101	_PLAN_COLS = (
   102	    "id, dag_id, run_id, parent_plan_id, json_body, status, "
   103	    "mutation_round, validation_errors, created_at, updated_at"
   104	)
   105

   107	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
   108	#  CRUD
   109	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

   111	
   112	async def _save_plan_impl(
   113	    conn, dag: DAG, run_id: Optional[str], parent_plan_id: Optional[int],
   114	    status: str, mutation_round: int,
   115	    validation_errors: Optional[list[DagValidationError]],
   116	) -> int:
   117	    now = time.time()
   118	    err_json = (
   119	        json.dumps([e.to_dict() for e in validation_errors])
   120	        if validation_errors else None
   121	    )
   122	    row = await conn.fetchrow(
   123	        "INSERT INTO dag_plans "
   124	        "(dag_id, run_id, parent_plan_id, json_body, status, "
   125	        "mutation_round, validation_errors, created_at, updated_at) "
   126	        "VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9) RETURNING id",
   127	        dag.dag_id, run_id, parent_plan_id, dag.model_dump_json(),
   128	        status, mutation_round, err_json, now, now,
   129	    )
   130	    return row["id"]
   131	
   132	
   133	async def save_plan(
   134	    dag: DAG, *,
   135	    run_id: Optional[str] = None,
   136	    parent_plan_id: Optional[int] = None,
   137	    status: str = "pending",
   138	    mutation_round: int = 0,
   139	    validation_errors: Optional[list[DagValidationError]] = None,
   140	    conn=None,
   141	) -> StoredPlan:
   142	    """Insert a fresh plan row. Use `set_status` for transitions."""
   143	    if status not in _VALID_STATUSES:
   144	        raise ValueError(f"unknown status {status!r}")
   145	    if conn is None:
   146	        async with get_pool().acquire() as owned:
   147	            new_id = await _save_plan_impl(
   148	                owned, dag, run_id, parent_plan_id,
   149	                status, mutation_round, validation_errors,
   150	            )
   151	    else:
   152	        new_id = await _save_plan_impl(
   153	            conn, dag, run_id, parent_plan_id,
   154	            status, mutation_round, validation_errors,
   155	        )
   156	    logger.info(
   157	        "dag plan saved id=%s dag=%s status=%s round=%d",
   158	        new_id, dag.dag_id, status, mutation_round,
   159	    )
   160	    return await get_plan(new_id, conn=conn)
   161	
   162	
   163	async def get_plan(plan_id: int, conn=None) -> StoredPlan:
   164	    sql = f"SELECT {_PLAN_COLS} FROM dag_plans WHERE id = $1"
   165	    if conn is None:
   166	        async with get_pool().acquire() as owned:
   167	            row = await owned.fetchrow(sql, plan_id)
   168	    else:
   169	        row = await conn.fetchrow(sql, plan_id)
   170	    if not row:
   171	        raise LookupError(f"no dag_plan id={plan_id}")
   172	    return _row_to_plan(row)
   173	
   174	
   175	async def get_plan_by_run(
   176	    run_id: str, conn=None,
   177	) -> Optional[StoredPlan]:
   178	    """Latest plan attached to a workflow_run (latest = highest id =
   179	    most recent mutation round)."""
   180	    sql = (
   181	        f"SELECT {_PLAN_COLS} FROM dag_plans WHERE run_id = $1 "
   182	        "ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
   183	    )
   184	    if conn is None:
   185	        async with get_pool().acquire() as owned:
   186	            row = await owned.fetchrow(sql, run_id)
   187	    else:
   188	        row = await conn.fetchrow(sql, run_id)
   189	    return _row_to_plan(row) if row else None

   191	
   192	async def list_plans(
   193	    dag_id: str, conn=None,
   194	) -> list[StoredPlan]:
   195	    """All plans for one logical DAG, ordered by mutation round."""
   196	    sql = (
   197	        f"SELECT {_PLAN_COLS} FROM dag_plans WHERE dag_id = $1 "
   198	        "ORDER BY mutation_round, id"
   199	    )
   200	    if conn is None:
   201	        async with get_pool().acquire() as owned:
   202	            rows = await owned.fetch(sql, dag_id)
   203	    else:
   204	        rows = await conn.fetch(sql, dag_id)
   205	    return [_row_to_plan(r) for r in rows]
   206	
   207	
   208	async def _set_status_impl(
   209	    conn, plan_id: int, new_status: str, run_id: Optional[str],
   210	) -> None:
   211	    # SELECT FOR UPDATE holds a row-level lock for the duration of the
   212	    # tx — concurrent set_status on the same plan_id serialise on the
   213	    # lock. Without it, two callers could both read status=validated,
   214	    # both compute "validated → executing is legal", both UPDATE; the
   215	    # double-transition is technically idempotent but produces
   216	    # surprising ordering in timestamps and logs.
   217	    row = await conn.fetchrow(
   218	        "SELECT status FROM dag_plans WHERE id = $1 FOR UPDATE",
   219	        plan_id,
   220	    )
   221	    if not row:
   222	        raise LookupError(f"no dag_plan id={plan_id}")
   223	    current = row["status"]
   224	    if new_status not in _ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[current]:
   225	        raise ValueError(
   226	            f"illegal transition {current!r} → {new_status!r} "
   227	            f"(allowed: {sorted(_ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[current])})"
   228	        )
   229	    now = time.time()
   230	    if run_id is not None:
   231	        await conn.execute(
   232	            "UPDATE dag_plans SET status = $1, run_id = $2, "
   233	            "updated_at = $3 WHERE id = $4",
   234	            new_status, run_id, now, plan_id,
   235	        )
   236	    else:
   237	        await conn.execute(
   238	            "UPDATE dag_plans SET status = $1, updated_at = $2 "
   239	            "WHERE id = $3",
   240	            new_status, now, plan_id,
   241	        )
   242	
   243	
   244	async def set_status(
   245	    plan_id: int, new_status: str, *,
   246	    run_id: Optional[str] = None, conn=None,
   247	) -> StoredPlan:
   248	    """Transition status; refuses illegal moves. Optionally attach
   249	    `run_id` (used when 'pending' → 'executing' to bind a new run).
   250	
   251	    SP-5.1 (2026-04-21): the SELECT-then-UPDATE is now wrapped in a
   252	    transaction with ``SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`` — under SQLite the
   253	    file-lock serialised callers implicitly; under asyncpg pool two
   254	    concurrent set_status on the same plan could both read the same
   255	    status and both issue UPDATEs, making the ordering ambiguous.
   256	    FOR UPDATE makes it deterministic.
   257	    """
   258	    if new_status not in _VALID_STATUSES:
   259	        raise ValueError(f"unknown status {new_status!r}")
   260	    if conn is None:
   261	        async with get_pool().acquire() as owned:
   262	            async with owned.transaction():
   263	                await _set_status_impl(owned, plan_id, new_status, run_id)
   264	    else:
   265	        async with conn.transaction():
   266	            await _set_status_impl(conn, plan_id, new_status, run_id)
   267	    return await get_plan(plan_id, conn=conn)
   268	
   269	
   270	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
   271	#  workflow_runs ↔ plan glue
   272	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
   273	
   274	
   275	async def _attach_to_run_impl(
   276	    conn, plan_id: int, run_id: str,
   277	) -> None:
   278	    now = time.time()
   279	    await conn.execute(
   280	        "UPDATE dag_plans SET run_id = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3",
   281	        run_id, now, plan_id,
   282	    )
   283	    await conn.execute(
   284	        "UPDATE workflow_runs SET dag_plan_id = $1 WHERE id = $2",
   285	        plan_id, run_id,
   286	    )
   287	
   288	
   289	async def attach_to_run(
   290	    plan_id: int, run_id: str, conn=None,
   291	) -> None:
   292	    """Two-way link: dag_plans.run_id ← run_id, workflow_runs.dag_plan_id ← plan_id.
   293	
   294	    SP-5.1: the two UPDATEs now land atomically. Previously a crash
   295	    between the two statements left dag_plans pointing at a run but
   296	    workflow_runs' dag_plan_id unset — the reverse lookup
   297	    ``get_dag_plan_id_for_run`` then returned None while the forward
   298	    lookup ``get_plan_by_run`` worked. The tx wrap makes the link
   299	    all-or-nothing.
   300	    """
   301	    if conn is None:
   302	        async with get_pool().acquire() as owned:
   303	            async with owned.transaction():
   304	                await _attach_to_run_impl(owned, plan_id, run_id)
   305	    else:
   306	        async with conn.transaction():
   307	            await _attach_to_run_impl(conn, plan_id, run_id)
   308	
   309	
   310	async def link_successor(
   311	    old_run_id: str, new_run_id: str, conn=None,
   312	) -> None:
   313	    """Mark the old workflow_run as superseded by `new_run_id`. Used
   314	    when DAG mutation forces a re-plan — Phase 56's append-only invariant
   315	    is preserved (we never edit the old run's steps), and the chain
   316	    stays traceable for audit/replay."""
   317	    sql = "UPDATE workflow_runs SET successor_run_id = $1 WHERE id = $2"
   318	    if conn is None:
   319	        async with get_pool().acquire() as owned:
   320	            await owned.execute(sql, new_run_id, old_run_id)
   321	    else:
   322	        await conn.execute(sql, new_run_id, old_run_id)
   323	
   324	
   325	async def get_dag_plan_id_for_run(
   326	    run_id: str, conn=None,
   327	) -> Optional[int]:
   328	    """Reverse lookup helper for any consumer that has a run_id and
   329	    needs the plan."""
   330	    sql = "SELECT dag_plan_id FROM workflow_runs WHERE id = $1"
   331	    if conn is None:
   332	        async with get_pool().acquire() as owned:
   333	            row = await owned.fetchrow(sql, run_id)
   334	    else:
   335	        row = await conn.fetchrow(sql, run_id)
   336	    if not row:
   337	        return None
   338	    return row["dag_plan_id"]

   152	def _headers() -> dict[str, str]:
   153	    # User-Agent is mandatory against CF-fronted prod — the default
   154	    # `Python-urllib/3.x` signature is flagged by Cloudflare Bot Fight
   155	    # Mode (Error 1010 "browser_signature_banned"). Ship an explicit
   156	    # named-tool UA so the smoke test is attributable in edge logs
   157	    # and passes reputation gates. Override via OMNISIGHT_SMOKE_UA if
   158	    # the operator ever needs to impersonate a specific client.
   159	    ua = os.environ.get(
   160	        "OMNISIGHT_SMOKE_UA",
   161	        "OmniSight-SmokeTest/1.0 (+https://github.com/limit5/OmniSight-Productizer)",
   162	    )
   163	    h = {
   164	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
   165	        "Accept": "application/json",
   166	        "User-Agent": ua,
   167	    }
   168	    if TOKEN:
   169	        h["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {TOKEN}"
   170	    # Optional session cookie for callers that already have one; no
   171	    # longer required since auth_baseline now recognises Bearer tokens
   172	    # directly (fixed 2026-04-24, see backend/auth_baseline.py
   173	    # _has_valid_bearer_token). Retained so an operator who wants to
   174	    # hit the smoke script from a logged-in browser context can still
   175	    # pass OMNISIGHT_SESSION_COOKIE without also provisioning an API
   176	    # token.
   177	    session = os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_SESSION_COOKIE", "").strip()
   178	    if session:
   179	        h["Cookie"] = f"omnisight_session={session}"
   180	    return h

   183	def _post(path: str, body: dict) -> dict:
   184	    url = f"{API}{path}"
   185	    data = json.dumps(body).encode()
   186	    req = request.Request(url, data=data, headers=_headers(), method="POST")
   187	    try:
   188	        with request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as resp:
   189	            return json.loads(resp.read())
   190	    except urlerror.HTTPError as exc:
   191	        resp_body = exc.read().decode(errors="replace")
   192	        print(f"  HTTP {exc.code}: {resp_body}", file=sys.stderr)
   193	        raise
   194	
   195	
   196	def _get(path: str) -> dict:
   197	    url = f"{API}{path}"
   198	    req = request.Request(url, headers=_headers(), method="GET")
   199	    with request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as resp:
   200	        return json.loads(resp.read())

   221	def poll_run(run_id: str) -> dict:
   222	    """Poll workflow run until terminal status or timeout."""
   223	    deadline = time.time() + POLL_TIMEOUT
   224	    while time.time() < deadline:
   225	        run = _get(f"/workflow/runs/{run_id}")
   226	        status = run.get("run", {}).get("status", run.get("status", "unknown"))
   227	        if status in ("completed", "failed", "halted"):
   228	            return run
   229	        print(f"  ... status={status}, waiting {POLL_INTERVAL}s")
   230	        time.sleep(POLL_INTERVAL)
   231	    print(f"  TIMEOUT after {POLL_TIMEOUT}s", file=sys.stderr)
   232	    return {}
   233

   235	# ── Verification ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   236	
   237	def verify_run(run_id: str, run_data: dict) -> list[str]:
   238	    """Return list of failures (empty = pass)."""
   239	    failures: list[str] = []
   240	    run_info = run_data.get("run", run_data)
   241	    status = run_info.get("status", "unknown")
   242	    if status != "completed":
   243	        failures.append(f"run status={status}, expected completed")
   244	
   245	    steps = run_data.get("steps", [])
   246	    if not steps:
   247	        failures.append("no steps recorded")
   248	    for s in steps:
   249	        if s.get("error"):
   250	            failures.append(f"step {s.get('key','?')} error: {s['error']}")
   251	        if not s.get("is_done"):
   252	            failures.append(f"step {s.get('key','?')} not done")
   253	
   254	    return failures
   255

   264	    except Exception as exc:
   265	        return False, f"audit verify request failed: {exc}"

   270	def generate_report(results: list[dict]) -> str:
   271	    lines = [
   272	        "## A2 L1-05 Prod Smoke Test Report",
   273	        "",
   274	        f"**Date**: {time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC', time.gmtime())}",
   275	        f"**Target**: {BASE_URL}",

   277	        "",
   278	    ]
   279	
   280	    all_pass = True
   281	    for r in results:
   282	        label = r["label"]
   283	        run_id = r.get("run_id", "N/A")
   284	        plan_id = r.get("plan_id", "N/A")
   285	        status = r.get("final_status", "unknown")
   286	        failures = r.get("failures", [])
   287	        passed = len(failures) == 0
   288	
   289	        if not passed:
   290	            all_pass = False
   291	
   292	        lines.append(f"### {label}")
   293	        lines.append(f"- **run_id**: `{run_id}`")
   294	        lines.append(f"- **plan_id**: `{plan_id}`")
   295	        lines.append(f"- **final_status**: `{status}`")
   296	        lines.append(f"- **result**: {'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL'}")
   297	        if failures:
   298	            for f in failures:
   299	                lines.append(f"  - {f}")
   300	        lines.append("")
   301	
   302	    audit_ok, audit_detail = verify_audit_chain()
   303	    lines.append("### Audit Hash-Chain Integrity")
   304	    lines.append(f"- **result**: {'PASS' if audit_ok else 'FAIL'}")
   305	    if audit_detail:
   306	        lines.append(f"- **detail**: {audit_detail}")
   307	    if not audit_ok:
   308	        all_pass = False
   309	    lines.append("")
   310	
   311	    lines.append(f"### Overall: {'ALL PASS' if all_pass else 'SOME FAILURES'}")
   312	    return "\n".join(lines)

   315	# ── Main ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   316	
   317	def main() -> int:
   318	    print("OmniSight Prod Smoke Test — A2 L1-05")
   319	    print(f"Target: {BASE_URL}")
   320	
   321	    results: list[dict] = []
   322	
   323	    for label, payload in DAGS:
   324	        try:
   325	            submit_result = submit_dag(label, payload)
   326	        except Exception as exc:
   327	            print(f"  SUBMIT FAILED: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
   328	            results.append({"label": label, "failures": [f"submit failed: {exc}"]})
   329	            continue
   330	
   331	        run_id = submit_result.get("run_id")
   332	        plan_id = submit_result.get("plan_id")
   333	        status = submit_result.get("status", "unknown")
   334	
   335	        if status == "failed" or not run_id:
   336	            errs = submit_result.get("validation_errors", [])
   337	            results.append({
   338	                "label": label,
   339	                "run_id": run_id,
   340	                "plan_id": plan_id,
   341	                "final_status": status,
   342	                "failures": [f"validation: {e}" for e in errs] or ["submit returned failed"],
   343	            })
   344	            continue
   345	
   346	        print(f"\n  Polling run {run_id} for completion...")
   347	        run_data = poll_run(run_id)
   348	        failures = verify_run(run_id, run_data) if run_data else ["poll timed out"]
   349	        final_status = run_data.get("run", {}).get("status", "timeout") if run_data else "timeout"
   350	
   351	        results.append({
   352	            "label": label,
   353	            "run_id": run_id,
   354	            "plan_id": plan_id,
   355	            "final_status": final_status,
   356	            "failures": failures,
   357	        })
   358	
   359	    report = generate_report(results)
   360	    print(f"\n{'='*60}")
   361	    print(report)
   362	    print(f"{'='*60}")
   363	
   364	    report_path = "data/smoke-test-report-a2.md"
   365	    os.makedirs("data", exist_ok=True)
   366	    with open(report_path, "w") as f:
   367	        f.write(report)
   368	    print(f"\nReport saved to {report_path}")
   369	
   370	    any_fail = any(r.get("failures") for r in results)
   371	    return 2 if any_fail else 0
   372	
   373	
   374	if __name__ == "__main__":
   375	    sys.exit(main())

   280	class VersionConflict(Exception):
   281	    """Raised when an optimistic-lock version check fails (HTTP 409)."""
   282	
   283	
   284	def _emit_workflow_updated_safe(run_id: str, status: str, version: int,
   285	                                kind: str | None = None) -> None:
   286	    """Q.3-SUB-1 (#297): fire emit_workflow_updated, swallowing errors.
   287	
   288	    Used by every workflow_runs UPDATE path (finish / cancel_run /
   289	    retry_run / update_run_metadata). A flaky SSE bus / Redis
   290	    outage must NEVER fail a committed workflow mutation — the
   291	    emit runs AFTER the version bump returned successfully, so
   292	    by this point the truth is in PG and the SSE push is purely a
   293	    UI-latency optimisation.
   294	    """
   295	    try:
   296	        from backend.events import emit_workflow_updated
   297	        emit_workflow_updated(run_id, status, version, kind=kind)
   298	    except Exception as exc:
   299	        logger.debug("emit_workflow_updated failed for %s: %s", run_id, exc)
   300	
   301	
   302	async def _bump_version(run_id: str, expected_version: int | None,
   303	                        updates: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
   304	    """Apply column updates to a workflow_run with optimistic locking.
   305	
   306	    Returns the new version.  Raises VersionConflict when the row's
   307	    current version doesn't match `expected_version`.  When
   308	    `expected_version` is None the check is skipped (internal callers).
   309	
   310	    SP-5.6a: now uses ``UPDATE ... RETURNING version`` so the
   311	    success/miss decision rides the same query (asyncpg doesn't
   312	    expose ``rowcount`` on Pool.execute the way the compat wrapper
   313	    did). RETURNING gives us the post-bump version directly, no
   314	    need to compute it from expected_version.
   315	    """
   316	    set_parts: list[str] = []
   317	    params: list[Any] = []
   318	    for col, val in updates.items():
   319	        params.append(val)
   320	        set_parts.append(f"{col} = ${len(params)}")
   321	    set_parts.append("version = version + 1")
   322	    if expected_version is not None:
   323	        params.append(run_id)
   324	        params.append(expected_version)
   325	        where = f"id = ${len(params) - 1} AND version = ${len(params)}"
   326	    else:
   327	        params.append(run_id)
   328	        where = f"id = ${len(params)}"
   329	    sql = (
   330	        f"UPDATE workflow_runs SET {', '.join(set_parts)} "
   331	        f"WHERE {where} RETURNING version"
   332	    )
   333	    async with get_pool().acquire() as conn:
   334	        row = await conn.fetchrow(sql, *params)
   335	    if row is None:
   336	        raise VersionConflict(run_id)
   337	    return int(row["version"])
   338	
   339	
   340	async def finish(run_id: str, status: RunStatus = "completed",
   341	                 expected_version: int | None = None) -> None:
   342	    new_version = await _bump_version(run_id, expected_version, {
   343	        "status": status,
   344	        "completed_at": time.time(),
   345	    })
   346	    _emit_workflow_updated_safe(run_id, status, new_version)
   347	
   348	    # Y9 #285 row 3 — fan one ``workflow_run`` billing event per
   349	    # finish() call into ``billing_usage_events`` so T4 can roll up
   350	    # run-counts + duration per ``(tenant_id, project_id)`` tuple.
   351	    # Cost is 0.0 — the LLM calls inside the run already wrote their
   352	    # own ``llm_call`` rows; this row is the run-count + duration
   353	    # billing signal only and never double-counts spend (Y9 row 3
   354	    # contract). Reads ``workflow_runs.tenant_id`` / ``project_id``
   355	    # directly off the row so we don't depend on the request-scope
   356	    # ContextVar still being set when ``finish`` is called from a
   357	    # background task. Best-effort — billing emit failure must never
   358	    # regress the workflow.finish contract.
   359	    try:
   360	        from backend import billing_usage as _billing
   361	        from backend.db_pool import get_pool as _get_pool
   362	        # Y9 #285 row 4 — LEFT JOIN ``projects`` so the billing fan-out
   363	        # carries ``product_line`` (column lives on projects, not
   364	        # workflow_runs). NULL ``project_id`` (legacy pre-Y1 rows) +
   365	        # missing project row both yield ``product_line = None`` which
   366	        # buckets to ``"unknown"`` in the Prometheus label without
   367	        # crashing the JOIN — LEFT JOIN keeps the workflow_run row even
   368	        # when the project is unknown / archived.
   369	        async with _get_pool().acquire() as _conn:
   370	            row = await _conn.fetchrow(
   371	                "SELECT wr.kind, wr.started_at, wr.completed_at, "
   372	                "wr.tenant_id, wr.project_id, p.product_line "
   373	                "FROM workflow_runs wr "
   374	                "LEFT JOIN projects p ON p.id = wr.project_id "
   375	                "WHERE wr.id = $1",
   376	                run_id,
   377	            )
   378	        if row is not None:
   379	            duration_ms: int | None = None
   380	            try:
   381	                if row["completed_at"] is not None and row["started_at"] is not None:
   382	                    duration_ms = int(
   383	                        (float(row["completed_at"]) - float(row["started_at"])) * 1000
   384	                    )
   385	            except Exception:
   386	                duration_ms = None
   387	            await _billing.record_workflow_run(
   388	                workflow_run_id=run_id,
   389	                workflow_kind=row["kind"] or "",
   390	                workflow_status=status,
   391	                duration_ms=duration_ms,
   392	                tenant_id=row["tenant_id"],
   393	                project_id=row["project_id"],
   394	                product_line=row["product_line"],
   395	            )
   396	    except Exception as exc:
   397	        logger.warning(
   398	            "billing_usage.record_workflow_run failed for run=%s: %s",
   399	            run_id, exc,
   400	        )
   401	
   402	    # Phase 62 hook: when a long / hard-fought run completes successfully
   403	    # and OMNISIGHT_SELF_IMPROVE_LEVEL includes L1, distil it into a
   404	    # skill candidate and file a Decision Engine proposal. Failures here
   405	    # must NEVER break the workflow.finish contract — wrap everything.
   406	    if status == "completed":
   407	        run = None
   408	        steps = None
   409	        try:
   410	            from backend import skills_extractor as _ex
   411	            if _ex.is_enabled():
   412	                run = await get_run(run_id)
   413	                steps = await list_steps(run_id)
   414	                if run is not None:
   415	                    result = _ex.extract(run, steps)
   416	                    if result.written:
   417	                        _ex.propose_promotion(result, run)
   418	        except Exception as exc:
   419	            logger.warning(
   420	                "skills extractor hook failed for run=%s: %s", run_id, exc,
   421	            )
   422	        try:
   423	            from backend import skill_distiller as _distiller
   424	            if _distiller.is_enabled():
   425	                if run is None:
   426	                    run = await get_run(run_id)
   427	                if steps is None:
   428	                    steps = await list_steps(run_id)
   429	                if run is not None:
   430	                    await _distiller.architect_guild_hook(run, steps)
   431	        except Exception as exc:
   432	            logger.warning(
   433	                "skill distiller hook failed for run=%s: %s", run_id, exc,
   434	            )
   435	
   436	
   437	async def cancel_run(run_id: str, expected_version: int) -> int:
   438	    """Cancel a running workflow. Returns the new version."""
   439	    new_version = await _bump_version(run_id, expected_version, {
   440	        "status": "halted",
   441	        "completed_at": time.time(),
   442	    })
   443	    _emit_workflow_updated_safe(run_id, "halted", new_version)
   444	    return new_version
   445	
   446	
   447	async def retry_run(run_id: str, expected_version: int) -> WorkflowRun:
   448	    """Reset a failed/halted run back to 'running' for retry.
   449	    Returns the updated WorkflowRun."""
   450	    await _bump_version(run_id, expected_version, {
   451	        "status": "running",
   452	        "completed_at": None,
   453	    })
   454	    run = await get_run(run_id)
   455	    assert run is not None
   456	    _emit_workflow_updated_safe(run.id, run.status, run.version, kind=run.kind)
   457	    return run
   458	
   459	
   460	async def update_run_metadata(run_id: str, expected_version: int,
   461	                              metadata: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
   462	    """Merge new metadata into the run with version guard."""
   463	    existing = await get_run(run_id)
   464	    if not existing:
   465	        raise ValueError(f"run {run_id} not found")
   466	    merged = sync_agent_type_guild_id({**existing.metadata, **metadata})
   467	    new_version = await _bump_version(run_id, expected_version, {
   468	        "metadata": json.dumps(merged),
   469	        "guild_id": guild_id_from_values(merged),
   470	    })
   471	    # Status is unchanged for metadata-only patches — emit so other
   472	    # devices still see the version bump (etag refresh).
   473	    _emit_workflow_updated_safe(run_id, existing.status, new_version,
   474	                                kind=existing.kind)
   475	    return new_version
   476	
   477	
   478	async def _get_step(run_id: str, idempotency_key: str) -> Optional[StepRecord]:
   479	    async with get_pool().acquire() as conn:
   480	        row = await conn.fetchrow(
   481	            f"SELECT {_STEP_COLS} FROM workflow_steps "
   482	            "WHERE run_id = $1 AND idempotency_key = $2",
   483	            run_id, idempotency_key,
   484	        )
   485	    return _row_to_step(row) if row else None
   486	
   487	
   488	async def _record_step(run_id: str, idempotency_key: str,
   489	                       output: Any, error: str | None) -> StepRecord:
   490	    """Atomically insert a finished step. UNIQUE constraint catches a
   491	    race with another writer — in which case we read back the existing
   492	    record and return that (last writer wins on raw run, but for
   493	    idempotency the cached output is what we want anyway).
   494	
   495	    SP-5.6a: INSERT + UPDATE now run inside a single tx so the
   496	    last_step_id bump on workflow_runs is committed with the
   497	    workflow_steps row (previously under compat a crash between
   498	    statements could leave the step recorded but last_step_id
   499	    pointing at a prior step — visible to replay() as an
   500	    inconsistency). On UNIQUE-violation we roll back explicitly via
   501	    the ``async with transaction()`` exception path and fall through
   502	    to the idempotency re-read.
   503	    """
   504	    step = StepRecord(
   505	        id=_uid("step"), run_id=run_id, idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
   506	        started_at=time.time(), completed_at=time.time(),
   507	        output=output, error=error,
   508	    )
   509	    try:
   510	        async with get_pool().acquire() as conn:
   511	            async with conn.transaction():
   512	                await conn.execute(
   513	                    "INSERT INTO workflow_steps "
   514	                    "(id, run_id, idempotency_key, started_at, "
   515	                    " completed_at, output_json, error) "
   516	                    "VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7)",
   517	                    step.id, step.run_id, step.idempotency_key,
   518	                    step.started_at, step.completed_at,
   519	                    json.dumps(output) if output is not None else None,
   520	                    error,
   521	                )
   522	                await conn.execute(
   523	                    "UPDATE workflow_runs SET last_step_id = $1 "
   524	                    "WHERE id = $2",
   525	                    step.id, run_id,
   526	                )
   527	        return step
   528	    except Exception as exc:
   529	        # Likely UNIQUE collision — the tx already rolled back.
   530	        if "unique" not in str(exc).lower():
   531	            logger.warning("workflow._record_step error: %s", exc)
   532	        existing = await _get_step(run_id, idempotency_key)
   533	        if existing:
   534	            return existing
   535	        raise
   536	
   537	
   538	def step(run: WorkflowRun, idempotency_key: str):
   539	    """Decorator: wrap an async callable so it only runs once per
   540	    (run, key). On second call with the same key, returns the cached
   541	    output without invoking the body.
   542	
   543	    Use distinct keys for genuinely separate work (`fetch_repo`,
   544	    `compile/<sha>`, `push/<branch>`); reuse the same key when you
   545	    want exactly-once semantics across crash-and-resume.
   546	    """
   547	    def decorator(fn: Callable[..., Awaitable[Any]]) -> Callable[..., Awaitable[Any]]:
   548	        async def wrapper(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
   549	            cached = await _get_step(run.id, idempotency_key)
   550	            if cached and cached.is_done:
   551	                logger.info("workflow.step cache-hit run=%s key=%s", run.id, idempotency_key)
   552	                return cached.output
   553	            try:
   554	                result = await fn(*args, **kwargs)
   555	            except Exception as exc:
   556	                # Record the failure (so we can audit / inspect)
   557	                await _record_step(run.id, idempotency_key, output=None,
   558	                                   error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc!s}"[:512])
   559	                raise
   560	            await _record_step(run.id, idempotency_key, output=result, error=None)

   318	        )

   321	    run = await wf.start("invoke", dag=dag, metadata=metadata, target_profile=target_profile)
   322	    plan = await _ds.get_plan_by_run(run.id)
   323	    if plan is None:
   324	        # Plan persistence failed inside workflow.start — return best-
   325	        # effort so the caller can retry.
   326	        return JSONResponse(
   327	            status_code=500,
   328	            content={"detail": "plan persistence failed",

   330	        )
   331

   335	        from backend import dag_planner as dp
   336	        # Before we attempt to mutate, drop any pre-warms that were
   337	        # speculatively launched against the prior draft — the
   338	        # replanned DAG will have different in-degree-0 tasks.
   339	        await _cancel_prewarm(reason="dag_mutated")

   351	                "mutation_rounds": len(loop.attempts),

   367	            },
   368	        )
   369

(b) **Sub-phase 3-B（backend ↔ pg-primary 網路）**：`docker-compose.prod.yml` declare `db_ha` external network + backend-a/b dual-home；hot-attach via `docker network connect` 不重啟；DNS `pg-primary` 從兩 replica 解得到；production image 內 `await asyncpg.connect(...)` live 打通 PG 16.13；commit `c0300fec`. (c) **Sub-phase 3-C 第一次 crash — 7 張整表 + 7 個 column alembic drift**：`alembic upgrade head` 後 migrate 立刻 `UndefinedColumnError workflow_runs.dag_plan_id`；發現 `backend/db.py::_migrate()` 在 SQLite 跑 44 個 ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN 沒有對應 alembic migration；live SQLite vs fresh PG column diff 挖出 `bootstrap_state / dag_plans / iq_runs / mfa_backup_codes / password_history / prompt_versions / user_mfa` 7 張整表 + `sessions.ua_hash / users.must_change_password / workflow_runs.{dag_plan_id,successor_run_id} / workflow_steps.dag_task_id / episodic_memory.{decayed_score,last_used_at}` 7 個 column 缺；F2 只鎖 table-level、alembic-vs-live column-level 是第三軸從沒守住。(d) **Sub-phase 3-C 第二次 crash — `event_log.id` 沒 IDENTITY**：`int(None)` on `reset_identity_sequences`；root cause：0001 baseline 寫 `INTEGER PRIMARY KEY` 沒 AUTOINCREMENT、`alembic_pg_compat._translate_autoincrement` 的 regex 只抓顯式 AUTOINCREMENT、PG 拿 plain int；app 的 `INSERT INTO event_log (event_type, ...)` 會 NOT NULL violation。(e) **Sub-phase 3-C 第三次 crash — `REAL` 精度截斷破 Merkle 鏈**：`exit 5 target_chain_ok=false first_bad_id=1 why=curr_hash`；per-row diff `audit_log.ts`：SQLite `1776547569.684335`、PG `1776547584.0` — **SQLite REAL binary64 (8-byte) vs PG REAL binary32 (4-byte)**、半精度截斷小數秒破 `sha256(prev_hash || canonical || str(round(ts,6)))`。(f) **P1 — alembic 0016 + shim + test 三件事一 commit**（`7d79e42e`、549 行）：`backend/alembic/versions/0016_pg_schema_sync.py` dialect-aware（PG 用 `IF NOT EXISTS` idempotent、SQLite 跳過 ALTER 避免 `_migrate()` 雙寫 duplicate column）+ `event_log.id` guarded `ADD GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY`（讀 `pg_attribute.attidentity` 判斷避免 double-ADD）；`alembic_pg_compat._translate_real` 加 `REAL → DOUBLE PRECISION`，DDL-only 預防（`_DDL_GUARD_RE` filter `CREATE TABLE`/`ALTER TABLE`/`ADD COLUMN` 避免參數字串誤傷）；`backend/tests/test_migrator_schema_coverage.py::test_alembic_pg_matches_sqlite_column_by_column` 跨 dialect column-parity 測試（`OMNI_TEST_PG_URL` env skip pattern、CI `pg-live-integration` job 會 exercise）；`DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE` 全量 re-upgrade 後所有 REAL 欄（30+ 欄）改 DOUBLE PRECISION、`migrate --truncate-target --json` **exit 0 / source_chain_ok=true / target_chain_ok=true / 1150 rows / 9 IDENTITY sequences reset**. (g) **Step 5 reassessment 發現真 blocker — backend/db.py 尚未 port**：`grep import aiosqlite` → 只 `db.py / tenant_secrets.py / db_connection.py / test_j5`；`grep from backend.db_connection` → 只 `db_connection.py / test_db_url`；**production code 零 import AsyncDBConnection**；結論：G4 marked `[x]` 時 runtime 接線為零、`[x]` 是「code merged」不是「prod runs」；照 Production Readiness Gate SOP 停 cutover、checkpoint 給 operator。**未動到的部分** — `backend/db.py` / `backend/tenant_secrets.py` / `backend/audit.py` / `backend/session_store.py` / 所有 production runtime code 0 diff（本 session 純 infra + alembic + 測試）；前端、Caddy、frontend、cloudflared 0 改動；**沒 flip `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL`**、沒 rolling restart 接 PG；prod 跟 Step 3 執行前 100% 一樣（SQLite）、`/readyz` 綠、audit_log 1115 rows 無損. **未做**（刻意、Production Readiness Gate SOP 停機約定）— cutover ceremony 沒開、T+5/T+10/T+30 監控沒跑（沒 cutover 可監控）、sqlite-matrix CI job 沒 archive、`backend/db.py` 80 個 call site 沒 touch. **設計考量** — (1) **停在 deployed-inactive 不硬衝**：Production Readiness Gate 明文「code in main + tests green + CI 通過 ≠ production runs」；硬 flip env 會 silent 沒效、operator 以為 cutover 其實沒；TODO.md A3 Phase 3 改拆成 3-Infra `[D]` + 3-Runtime `[ ]` 誠實反映. (3) **`REAL → DOUBLE PRECISION` shim rewrite 只在 DDL context**：regex `\bREAL\b` 全局替換理論上參數字串含 'REAL' 會誤傷；用 `_DDL_GUARD_RE` 預先 filter. (5) **alembic 跑法 — production image + bind-mount**：既有 image 尚未 rebuild 含 0016 + shim 修；migration 是 offline DDL 不碰 runtime、bind-mount 迭代 low-risk；真 cutover 前要 rebuild baked-in.    107	def get_topology_mode() -> str:
   108	    """Return the graph topology feature-flag mode.
   109	
   110	    Unknown values fail closed to ``legacy`` so a typo does not activate
   111	    the BP.C S/M/XL runtime path before operators intend it.
   112	    """
   113	    mode = (os.getenv(TOPOLOGY_MODE_ENV) or "legacy").strip().lower()
   114	    if mode in VALID_TOPOLOGY_MODES:
   115	        return mode
   116	    return "legacy"

   121	    if size == "M":

   123	    return build_topology(size)

   126	def _route_after_orchestrator_with_guilds(state: GraphState) -> str:
   127	    """Route to conversation, a legacy specialist, or a mounted Guild node."""

   133	def _build_m_guild_topology():
   134	    """Build the M topology with BP.B Guild nodes mounted.
   135	
   136	    The legacy specialist nodes stay mounted for backward compatibility
   137	    during the BP.B dual-write window. Guild slugs are also valid nodes so
   138	    BP.B.4 routing can hand off to Guild names without another graph-level
   139	    change.
   140	    """
   141	    builder = StateGraph(GraphState)
   142	
   143	    builder.add_node("orchestrator", orchestrator_node)
   144	    for name, node in _LEGACY_NODE_FACTORIES.items():
   145	        builder.add_node(name, node)
   146	    for guild_name in GUILD_GRAPH_NODES:
   147	        if guild_name not in _LEGACY_NODE_FACTORIES:
   148	            builder.add_node(guild_name, _specialist_node_factory(guild_name))
   149	    builder.add_node("tool_executor", tool_executor_node)
   150	    builder.add_node("error_check", error_check_node)
   151	    builder.add_node("conversation", conversation_node)
   152	    builder.add_node("context_gate", context_compression_gate)
   153	    builder.add_node("summarizer", summarizer_node)
   154	
   155	    builder.set_entry_point("orchestrator")
   156	    route_targets = {
   157	        "conversation": "conversation",
   158	        **{name: name for name in (*STANDARD_SPECIALISTS, *GUILD_GRAPH_NODES)},
   159	    }
   160	    builder.add_conditional_edges(
   161	        "orchestrator",
   162	        _route_after_orchestrator_with_guilds,
   163	        route_targets,
   164	    )
   165	    builder.add_edge("conversation", "context_gate")
   166	
   167	    for node_name in route_targets:
   168	        if node_name == "conversation":
   169	            continue
   170	        builder.add_conditional_edges(
   171	            node_name,
   172	            _check_tool_calls,
   173	            {
   174	                "tool_executor": "tool_executor",
   175	                "context_gate": "context_gate",
   176	            },
   177	        )
   178	
   179	    builder.add_edge("tool_executor", "error_check")
   180	    builder.add_conditional_edges(
   181	        "error_check",
   182	        _should_retry,
   183	        {
   184	            **{name: name for name in (*STANDARD_SPECIALISTS, *GUILD_GRAPH_NODES)},
   185	            "summarizer": "context_gate",
   186	        },
   187	    )
   188	    builder.add_edge("context_gate", "summarizer")
   189	    builder.add_edge("summarizer", END)
   190	
   191	    return builder.compile()
   192	
   193	
   194	# Singleton compiled graphs.
   195	#
   196	# Module-global / cross-worker audit: the mapping is read-only after import and
   197	# each uvicorn worker compiles the same three graphs from code. No mutable
   198	# cache, singleton mutation, or cross-worker coordination is required.
   199	_TOPOLOGY_GRAPHS = MappingProxyType({
   200	    size: build_graph(size) for size in VALID_TOPOLOGY_SIZES
   201	})
   202	
   203	# Backward-compatible alias used by existing imports/tests: legacy == M.
   204	agent_graph = _TOPOLOGY_GRAPHS["M"]
   205	
   206	
   207	def _select_graph_for_state(state: GraphState):
   208	    """Pick the compiled graph for a run-scoped ``GraphState``."""
   209	    if get_topology_mode() != "smxl":
   210	        return agent_graph
   211	    return _TOPOLOGY_GRAPHS[state.size]
   212	
   213	
   214	# Hard upper bound on a single graph execution, in seconds. Long enough
   215	# to cover legitimate multi-tool reasoning runs; short enough to free a
   216	# worker before queues back up under load. Expressed as ``5 * 60`` so the
   217	# 5-minute intent is evident from the literal, not just the comment.
   218	GRAPH_TIMEOUT = 5 * 60
   219	
   220	
   221	async def _enforce_user_facing_firewall(
   222	    user_command: str,
   223	    *,
   224	    firewall_result: FirewallResult | None = None,
   225	    task_id: str | None = None,
   226	):
   227	    """Run KS.4.12's user-facing firewall before specialist routing.
   228	
   229	    Module-global / cross-worker audit: this helper stores no mutable state.
   230	    Each worker derives the same guard decision from request text plus the
   231	    configured classifier credentials at call time; internal graph node retries
   232	    do not re-enter this function.
   233	    """
   234	    from backend.config import settings
   235	
   236	    try:
   237	        return await enforce_input(
   238	            user_command,
   239	            result=firewall_result,
   240	            api_key=getattr(settings, "anthropic_api_key", "") or None,
   241	            actor="orchestrator_entry",
   242	            entity_id=task_id,
   243	            session_id=task_id,
   244	        )
   245	    except RuntimeError as exc:
   246	        # Fresh dev/test installs often have no firewall classifier key.
   247	        # Keep the entry point callable while production deployments with
   248	        # configured credentials still enforce through Haiku.
   249	        if "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" not in str(exc):
   250	            raise
   251	        return None
   252	
   253	
   254	async def run_graph(
   255	    user_command: str,
   256	    workspace_path: str | None = None,
   257	    model_name: str = "",
   258	    agent_sub_type: str = "",
   259	    handoff_context: str = "",
   260	    task_skill_context: str = "",

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""O3 (#266) — Stateless Agent Worker Pool.
     2	
     3	A ``Worker`` is the small, long-lived process that turns CATC payloads
     4	sitting on the queue (O2 / ``backend/queue_backend.py``) into actual
     5	sandbox-executed agent runs that get committed and pushed to Gerrit
     6	for human review.  Workers are stateless: any number can run on any
     7	host, all draining the same shared queue.  Crash-safety comes from
     8	upstream:
     9	
    10	  * **Queue visibility timeout** (O2) — if the worker dies mid-task,
    11	    the message is automatically re-queued by ``sweep_visibility()``.
    12	  * **Distributed file-path lock** (O1) — held leases auto-expire if
    13	    the worker stops sending heartbeats; another worker takes the
    14	    file scope.
    15	  * **Redis ``workers:active`` set + per-worker heartbeat key with
    16	    TTL** — the operator surface (and, later, the orchestrator) can
    17	    spot a dead worker within 45 s (post-C4 audit 2026-04-19; previously
    18	    90 s). Note this is the *registry* TTL, not file-path dist-lock TTL.
    19	    File-path locks still expire on their own 30-min schedule; a
    20	    dead-worker lock cleanup cron is a follow-up row.
    21	
    22	The runtime stays pluggable so unit tests don't need real Docker or
    23	Gerrit:
    24	
    25	  * ``AgentExecutor`` — runs the actual agent step inside a sandbox
    26	    workspace and returns a ``AgentResult`` (commit message + files
    27	    touched).  Default implementation is a stub that just records the
    28	    invocation; production wires this to ``backend/container.py``.
    29	  * ``SandboxRuntime`` — provisions a workspace directory whose
    30	    bind-mount surface is restricted to the CATC card's
    31	    ``impact_scope.allowed`` paths and tears it down after.  Default
    32	    is the local-fs implementation (``LocalSandboxRuntime``); prod
    33	    can swap in ``DockerSandboxRuntime`` (uses ``container.py``).
    34	  * ``GerritPusher`` — pushes the resulting commit to
    35	    ``refs/for/main`` and records the Change-Id.  Default is a
    36	    no-op echo; prod uses ``backend/git_review.push_for_review``.
    37	
    38	CLI::
    39	
    40	    python -m backend.worker run \
    41	        --capacity 2 \
    42	        --tenant-filter t-acme \
    43	        --capability-filter firmware,vision
    44	
    45	Send SIGTERM to gracefully drain (stop claiming new work, finish
    46	in-flight tasks, release locks, deregister) — same signal both the
    47	systemd unit and the docker-compose profile use.
    48	"""
    49

    84	# worker drops from the `workers:active` registry inside 45 s instead of
    85	# 90 s. Refresh interval stays at TTL/3 (1.5x headroom for missed pings).
    86	# Note: this is ONLY the registry heartbeat — file-path dist-locks still
    87	# expire on DEFAULT_LOCK_TTL_S (30 min). A follow-up row should add a
    88	# dead-worker lock-release cron that keys off this registry TTL so a
    89	# SIGKILL'd worker's paths are reclaimable within ~45 s, not 30 min.
    90	DEFAULT_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_S = 15      # refresh every 15 s
    91	DEFAULT_HEARTBEAT_TTL_S = 45           # 3x interval — 2 missed pings = dead
    92	DEFAULT_VISIBILITY_TIMEOUT_S = 5 * 60  # spec: workers ack within 5 min

   109	@dataclass
   110	class AgentResult:
   111	    """What an ``AgentExecutor`` returns after running one task.
   112	
   113	    ``ok=True`` means the agent produced a coherent commit; the worker
   114	    will then ``commit + push`` it to Gerrit.  ``ok=False`` triggers a
   115	    ``nack`` with the rendered ``reason``/``stack`` (so the queue's
   116	    3-strike rule + DLQ policy stays the source of truth for "give up").
   117	    """
   118	
   119	    ok: bool
   120	    commit_message: str = ""
   121	    files_touched: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   122	    reason: str = ""
   123	    stack: str | None = None
   124	    extra: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
   125	
   126	
   127	@dataclass
   128	class GerritPushResult:
   129	    """Outcome of pushing a worker's commit to Gerrit."""
   130	
   131	    ok: bool
   132	    change_id: str = ""
   133	    review_url: str = ""
   134	    attempts: int = 1
   135	    reason: str = ""
   136	
   137	
   138	@dataclass
   139	class WorkerTaskOutcome:
   140	    """Per-message audit trail produced by a single ``Worker.handle()``."""
   141	
   142	    message_id: str
   143	    task_id: str
   144	    jira_ticket: str
   145	    status: str                          # acked | nacked | dlq | locked_skipped
   146	    agent_result: AgentResult | None = None
   147	    gerrit: GerritPushResult | None = None
   148	    error: str | None = None
   149	    elapsed_s: float = 0.0
   150	
   151	
   152	@dataclass
   153	class WorkerConfig:
   154	    """All knobs a ``Worker`` accepts.  Construct from CLI or fixtures."""
   155	
   156	    worker_id: str
   157	    capacity: int = DEFAULT_CAPACITY
   158	    tenant_filter: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   159	    capability_filter: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   160	    heartbeat_interval_s: float = DEFAULT_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_S
   161	    heartbeat_ttl_s: int = DEFAULT_HEARTBEAT_TTL_S
   162	    visibility_timeout_s: float = DEFAULT_VISIBILITY_TIMEOUT_S
   163	    loop_idle_s: float = DEFAULT_LOOP_IDLE_S
   164	    lock_wait_s: float = DEFAULT_LOCK_WAIT_S
   165	    lock_ttl_s: float = DEFAULT_LOCK_TTL_S
   166	    max_messages: int | None = None     # tests / one-shot drain
   167	    pull_count: int | None = None       # default = capacity
   168	    project_root: Path = field(default_factory=lambda: Path.cwd())
   169	
   170	    def effective_pull_count(self) -> int:
   171	        return max(1, self.pull_count if self.pull_count is not None
   172	                   else self.capacity)
   173	
   174	
   175	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
   176	#  Pluggable runtime protocols
   177	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
   178	
   179	
   180	class SandboxHandle:
   181	    """Opaque per-task handle returned from ``SandboxRuntime.start``.
   182	
   183	    Carries the workspace directory + any runtime-specific bookkeeping
   184	    needed for ``execute`` / ``stop``.
   185	    """
   186	
   187	    def __init__(self, workspace: Path, runtime_id: str = "",
   188	                 metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
   189	        self.workspace = workspace
   190	        self.runtime_id = runtime_id
   191	        self.metadata: dict[str, Any] = metadata or {}
   192	
   193	
   194	class SandboxRuntime(Protocol):
   195	    """Workspace + execution sandbox for a single task.
   196	
   197	    Implementations MUST honour ``allowed_paths``: nothing outside that
   198	    set should be physically reachable from inside the sandbox.  The
   199	    default ``LocalSandboxRuntime`` enforces this by copying-only the
   200	    allowed paths into a tmp workspace and refusing the launch when an
   201	    allowed glob escapes the project root.
   202	    """
   203	
   204	    def start(self, *, worker_id: str, task_id: str,
   205	              card: TaskCard, project_root: Path) -> SandboxHandle: ...
   206	
   207	    def commit(self, handle: SandboxHandle, *,
   208	               commit_message: str) -> str: ...
   209	
   210	    def stop(self, handle: SandboxHandle) -> None: ...
   211	
   212	
   213	class AgentExecutor(Protocol):
   214	    """Runs the agent step inside a prepared sandbox workspace."""
   215	
   216	    def run(self, *, handle: SandboxHandle, card: TaskCard,
   217	            worker_id: str) -> AgentResult: ...
   218	
   219	
   220	class GerritPusher(Protocol):
   221	    """Pushes a sandbox commit out for review."""
   222	
   223	    def push(self, *, handle: SandboxHandle, card: TaskCard,
   224	             commit_sha: str, change_id: str,
   225	             worker_id: str) -> GerritPushResult: ...
   226	
   227	
   228	class HeartbeatStore(Protocol):
   229	    """Backs ``worker:<id>:alive`` + ``workers:active`` set.
   230	
   231	    The default implementation prefers Redis when ``OMNISIGHT_REDIS_URL``
   232	    is set; otherwise falls back to an in-memory dict so single-process
   233	    tests / dev runs work without Redis.
   234	    """
   235	
   236	    def register(self, worker_id: str, info: dict[str, Any],
   237	                 ttl_s: int) -> None: ...
   238	
   239	    def heartbeat(self, worker_id: str, info: dict[str, Any],
   240	                  ttl_s: int) -> bool: ...
   241	
   242	    def deregister(self, worker_id: str) -> None: ...
   243	
   244	    def list_active(self) -> list[str]: ...
   245	
   246	    def get_info(self, worker_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None: ...
   247	
   248	
   249	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
   250	#  Default runtime implementations
   251	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
   252

    18	;   * OMNISIGHT_REDIS_URL          — required for shared queue + locks.
