   128	def iso_z(dt: datetime) -> str:
   129	    """ISO-8601 with a literal ``Z`` — matches ``scripts/canary_pipeline.py``
   130	    and is parsed by ``release_milestone_checker.parse_timestamp``."""
   131	    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
   132	
   133	
   134	def make_run_id(suite: str, dt: datetime) -> str:
   135	    return f"staging-{suite}-{dt.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')}"
   136	
   137	
   138	# ───────────────────────── pure record helpers ──────────────────────────
   139	
   140	
   141	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   142	class GateRecord:
   143	    suite: str
   144	    status: str
   145	    revision: str
   146	    run_id: str
   147	    timestamp: str
   148	    detail: str
   149	    evidence: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   150	
   151	    def to_jsonl_obj(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   152	        obj = {
   153	            "suite": self.suite,
   154	            "status": self.status,
   155	            "branch": BRANCH,
   156	            "revision": self.revision,
   157	            "run_id": self.run_id,
   158	            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
   159	            "detail": self.detail,
   160	        }
   161	        if self.evidence:
   162	            obj.update(self.evidence)
   163	        return obj
   164	
   165	
   166	def build_record(
   167	    *,
   168	    suite: str,
   169	    ok: bool,
   170	    detail: str,
   171	    revision: str,
   172	    run_id: str,
   173	    now: datetime,
   174	    evidence: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   175	) -> GateRecord:
   176	    return GateRecord(
   177	        suite=suite,
   178	        status=STATUS_GREEN if ok else STATUS_RED,
   179	        revision=revision,
   180	        run_id=run_id,
   480	
   481	
   482	# ───────────────────────── CLI ──────────────────────────────────────────
   483	
   484	
   485	def _build_canary_probe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> SuiteProbe:
   486	    paths = tuple(
   487	        p.strip() for p in (args.canary_paths or "").split(",") if p.strip()
   488	    ) or DEFAULT_CANARY_PATHS
   489	    return lambda: http_probe(
   490	        base_url=args.base_url,
   491	        paths=paths,
   492	        attempts=args.canary_attempts,
   493	        interval=args.canary_interval,
   494	        timeout=args.http_timeout,
   495	        opener=_default_http_opener,
   496	        sleeper=time.sleep,
   497	    )
   498	
   499	
   500	def _build_smoke_probe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> SuiteProbe:
   501	    return lambda: smoke_probe(
   502	        base_url=args.base_url,
   503	        subset=args.smoke_subset,
   504	        timeout=args.smoke_timeout,
   505	        runner=subprocess.run,
   506	    )
   507	
   508	
   509	def _build_evidence_probe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> EvidenceProbe:
   510	    return lambda: api_version_evidence(
   511	        base_url=args.base_url,
   512	        timeout=args.http_timeout,
   513	        opener=_default_http_opener,
   514	    )
   515	
   516	
   517	def _emit_status_line(record: GateRecord) -> None:
   518	    """Print a single structured JSON line to stdout (the systemd unit appends
   519	    it to a dedicated log; mirrors ``scripts/canary_pipeline.emit_status``)."""
   520	    obj = {
   521	        "timestamp": record.timestamp,
   522	        "level": "INFO" if record.status == STATUS_GREEN else "DEGRADED",
   523	        "event": f"staging_gate_{record.suite}_{record.status}",
   524	        **record.to_jsonl_obj(),
   525	    }
   526	    print(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True), flush=True)
   527	
   528	
   529	def build_arg_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
   530	    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
   531	    p.add_argument("--suite", choices=SUITES, required=True)
   532	    p.add_argument(
   533	        "--base-url",
   534	        default=os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_STAGING_URL", DEFAULT_STAGING_URL),
   535	        help="staging deployment base URL (default: $OMNISIGHT_STAGING_URL or %(default)s)",
   536	    )
   537	    p.add_argument(
   538	        "--out",
   539	        type=Path,
   540	        default=None,
   541	        help="JSONL file to append to (default: per-suite checker path / env)",
   542	    )
   543	    p.add_argument(
   544	        "--revision",
   545	        default=None,
   546	        help="develop-tip SHA to stamp (default: query Gerrit). Set by CI when "
   547	        "it already knows the tip it built.",
   548	    )
   549	    # Gerrit SSH (only used when --revision is not given). Same env var names
   550	    # as release_milestone_checker / the systemd release units.
   551	    p.add_argument("--gerrit-host", default=os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_GERRIT_SSH_HOST", "codex-bot@sora.services"))
   552	    p.add_argument("--gerrit-port", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_GERRIT_SSH_PORT", "29418")))
   553	    p.add_argument("--gerrit-project", default=os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_GERRIT_PROJECT", DEFAULT_GERRIT_PROJECT))
   554	    p.add_argument(
   555	        "--gerrit-key",
   556	        type=Path,
   557	        default=Path(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_GIT_SSH_KEY_PATH", "~/.config/omnisight/gerrit-codex-bot-ed25519")).expanduser(),
   558	    )
   559	    # Canary knobs.
   560	    p.add_argument("--canary-paths", default=os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_STAGING_CANARY_PATHS", ",".join(DEFAULT_CANARY_PATHS)))
   561	    p.add_argument("--canary-attempts", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_STAGING_CANARY_ATTEMPTS", str(DEFAULT_CANARY_ATTEMPTS))))
   562	    p.add_argument("--canary-interval", type=float, default=float(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_STAGING_CANARY_INTERVAL", str(DEFAULT_CANARY_INTERVAL_SECONDS))))
   563	    p.add_argument("--http-timeout", type=float, default=float(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_STAGING_HTTP_TIMEOUT", str(DEFAULT_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS))))
   564	    # Smoke knobs.
   565	    p.add_argument("--smoke-subset", default=os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_STAGING_SMOKE_SUBSET", DEFAULT_SMOKE_SUBSET))
   566	    p.add_argument("--smoke-timeout", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_STAGING_SMOKE_TIMEOUT", str(DEFAULT_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS))))
   567	    p.add_argument("--no-audit", action="store_true", help="skip the §6 release-audit write (for local dry runs)")
   568	    return p
   569	
   570	
   571	def _default_out_path(suite: str) -> Path:
   572	    if suite == SUITE_CANARY:
   573	        return Path(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_RELEASE_CANARY_LOG", str(DEFAULT_CANARY_LOG)))
   574	    return Path(os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_RELEASE_SMOKE_LOG", str(DEFAULT_SMOKE_LOG)))
   575	
   576	
   577	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
   578	    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s %(message)s")
   579	    args = build_arg_parser().parse_args(argv)
   580	
   581	    out_path = args.out or _default_out_path(args.suite)
   582	
   583	    revision = args.revision
   584	    if not revision:
   585	        try:
   586	            revision = resolve_develop_tip(
   587	                runner=subprocess.run,
   588	                host=args.gerrit_host,
   589	                port=args.gerrit_port,
   590	                key_path=args.gerrit_key if args.gerrit_key else None,
   591	                project=args.gerrit_project,
   592	            )
   593	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   594	            log.error("cannot resolve develop tip from Gerrit: %s", exc)
   595	            return 3
   596	
   597	    probe = _build_canary_probe(args) if args.suite == SUITE_CANARY else _build_smoke_probe(args)
   598	    audit_sink: AuditSink = (lambda *_a, **_k: None) if args.no_audit else _write_audit
   599	
   600	    result = run_gate(
   601	        suite=args.suite,
   602	        revision=revision,
   603	        probe=probe,
   604	        out_path=out_path,
   605	        audit_sink=audit_sink,
   606	        evidence_probe=_build_evidence_probe(args),
   607	    )
   608	    if result.record is not None:
   609	        _emit_status_line(result.record)
   610	    return result.exit_code
   611	
   612	
   613	if __name__ == "__main__":  # pragma: no cover
   614	    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

   306	
   307	
   308	def api_version_evidence(
   309	    *,
   310	    base_url: str,
   311	    timeout: float,
   312	    opener: HttpOpener,
   313	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   314	    """Observe staging ``/api/version`` and extract RT-05d JSONL evidence."""
   315	    url = base_url.rstrip("/") + "/api/version"
   316	    status, body = opener(url, timeout)
   317	    if not (200 <= status < 300):
   318	        raise RuntimeError(f"/api/version: HTTP {status}")
   319	    try:
   320	        observed = json.loads(body)
   321	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   322	        raise RuntimeError(f"/api/version returned invalid JSON: {exc}") from exc
   323	    if not isinstance(observed, dict):
   324	        raise RuntimeError("/api/version returned non-object JSON")
   325	
   326	    bundle_id = observed.get("bundle_id")
   327	    # Prefer the RT-08 overlay's deployed_digest_* (the real post-build digests
   328	    # the deployed backend actually serves). The image-baked *_image_digest
   329	    # fields are zero placeholders — an image cannot embed its own digest at
   330	    # build time — and would fail promote_image_bundle.py's digest-equality gate.
   331	    backend_digest = observed.get("deployed_digest_backend") or observed.get("backend_image_digest")
   332	    frontend_digest = observed.get("deployed_digest_frontend") or observed.get("frontend_image_digest")
   333	    missing = [
   334	        name for name, value in (
   335	            ("bundle_id", bundle_id),
   336	            ("backend_image_digest", backend_digest),
   337	            ("frontend_image_digest", frontend_digest),
   338	        )
   339	        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value
   340	    ]
   341	    if missing:
   342	        raise RuntimeError(
   343	            "/api/version missing staging evidence field(s): " + ",".join(missing)
   344	        )
   345	    return {
   346	        "bundle_id": bundle_id,
   347	        "backend_digest": backend_digest,
   348	        "frontend_digest": frontend_digest,
   349	        "observed_api_version": observed,
   350	    }
   351	
   352	
   353	def smoke_probe(
   354	    *,
   355	    base_url: str,
   356	    subset: str,
   357	    timeout: int,
   358	    runner: Runner,
   359	    script_path: Path | None = None,
   360	    python_exe: str = sys.executable,
   361	) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   362	    """Run ``scripts/prod_smoke_test.py`` against the staging base URL.
   363	
   364	    Exit 0 → green. Any non-zero exit (1 = DAG submit failed, 2 = verification
   365	    failed) or a launch failure → red, with a trailing slice of the script's
   366	    output as the detail so the alert is actionable.
   367	    """
   368	    script = script_path or (REPO_ROOT / "scripts" / "prod_smoke_test.py")
   369	    argv = [python_exe, str(script), base_url.rstrip("/"), "--subset", subset]
   370	    try:
   371	        proc = runner(argv, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, check=False)
   372	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   373	        return False, f"prod_smoke_test.py timed out after {timeout}s"
   374	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   375	        return False, f"prod_smoke_test.py failed to launch: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
   376	    tail = ((proc.stdout or "") + (proc.stderr or "")).strip().splitlines()[-8:]
   377	    tail_text = " | ".join(tail)
   378	    if proc.returncode == 0:
   379	        return True, f"prod_smoke_test.py --subset {subset} passed"
   380	    return False, f"prod_smoke_test.py exit {proc.returncode}: {tail_text}"
   381	
   382	
   383	# ───────────────────────── audit sink (§6) ──────────────────────────────
   384	
   385	
   386	def _write_audit(action: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   387	    """Best-effort release-audit row. Mirrors
   388	    ``backend.agents.auto_promote_main._write_audit`` — fire ``backend.audit
   389	    .log`` on a fresh event loop, swallow everything. NEVER raises and never
   390	    blocks the gate run; an unreachable DB just means no audit row."""
   391	
   392	    async def _run() -> None:
   393	        from backend import audit
   394	
   395	        await audit.log(
   396	            action=action,
   397	            entity_kind="release_gate",
   398	            entity_id=str(payload.get("suite") or "staging"),
   399	            before=None,
   400	            after=payload,
   401	            actor="staging_gate",
   402	        )
   403	
   404	    try:
   405	        asyncio.get_running_loop()
   406	    except RuntimeError:
   407	        pass
   408	    else:
   409	        log.debug("staging-gate audit skipped: event loop already running")
   410	        return
   411	    try:
   412	        asyncio.run(_run())
   413	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
   414	        log.warning("staging-gate audit write failed for %s", action, exc_info=True)
   415	
   416

   421	class GateRunResult:
   422	    exit_code: int
   423	    record: GateRecord | None
   424	    detail: str
   425

   427	def run_gate(
   428	    *,
   429	    suite: str,
   430	    revision: str,

    57	
    58	The 2026-05-14 §2 lays out what a complete OmniSight must deliver. **Two-axis honest read** 2026-05-20: separate "bricks built" from "orchestration glue built" — because a system can have 60% of the pieces and still 0% of the end-to-end user flow.
    59	
    60	#### Layer interpretation per dimension
    61	
    62	| Dimension | Bricks (libraries / components / templates / configs) | Orchestration glue (user-input → output flow) | End-to-end user demo runs? |
    63	|---|---|---|---|
    64	| **2.1 Platform primitives** (tenant, identity, observability, governance, daemon supervision, cost ledger) | **~70%** built — `tenant_id` in 10+ backend files, 5 daemons + 19 timers, ADR-0001 governance, cosign keyed sign, OP-1478 image pipeline. **Cost ledger NOT extracted** (Phase 2 of strategic reframe) | ~70% (no user flow needed — these are platform-level) | ✅ for dev-runner internal use; ⛔ for user-product use |
    65	| **2.2 Domain skill packs** (Mobile first; per-vertical specialized agent + knowledge + workflow) | **~50-60%** built — `backend/{nextjs,nuxt,astro,fastapi,go_service,rust_cli,ios,android,flutter,rn}_scaffolder.py` ship `render_project()`; 10+ `configs/skills/` packs (enterprise_web, ipcam, mcp-builder, imaging, depth_sensing, etc.); `backend/figma_to_mobile.py` + `app_store_connect.py` + MP.W0/W1 mobile pipeline shipped; BP.A 4-template factory (Spec/Task/Impl/Review + Cognitive Load Scanner, 526 tests) | **~0-5%** — `git grep render_project` returns ONLY the scaffolders themselves; no third-party caller; no orchestrator wires user input to scaffolder dispatch | ⛔ no user can drive any scaffolder |
    66	| **2.3 UI / UX** (idea input, progress tracking, decision support, exception handling) | **~40-50%** built — `app/onboarding/page.tsx` 4-step wizard, `new-project-wizard.tsx` 4 starting modes (GitHub / Upload Docs / Describe in Prose / Blank DAG), `intention-picker.tsx` 4 intentions (incl. `web_app_generation`), tenant/project switcher, /admin/tenants page, WP.4 + WP.10 (BP Fleet UI Lanes), Y6 dashboard tour | **~5-10%** — intention picker sets a localStorage + JSON preference but no backend listener dispatches; new-project-wizard 4 modes route to UI panels but spec→artifact pipeline missing | ⛔ user can configure intent but cannot trigger generation |
    67	| **2.4 Compliance** (FCC/CE/KCC/VCCI/SRRC/NCC + RoHS/REACH/GDPR/export-control) | **~25%** built — `backend/mobile_compliance/` (ASC + Play + Privacy Label gates, 99 tests), `backend/web_compliance/` (W5), 184 cross-suite compliance tests | ~0% — runs only when invoked by dev pipeline, no user-facing compliance workflow | ⛔ |
    68	| **2.5 Supply chain** (BOM, JLCPCB/LCSC/Mouser/Digi-Key) | **0%** | 0% | ⛔ ([[project_hw_expansion_plan]] hard-deferred) |
    69	| **2.6 Manufacturing** | **0%** | 0% | ⛔ |
    70	| **2.7 Simulation / Verification** | **0%** | 0% | ⛔ |
    71	
    72	#### Aggregate honest read
    73	
    74	- **Bricks aggregate**: ~3.0-3.5 / 7 dimensions have substantial library-level implementation
    75	- **Orchestration aggregate**: ~1.0-1.5 / 7 dimensions have end-user-driven flow (mostly only 2.1 Platform primitives where dev-runner IS the user)
    76	- **End-to-end product demo**: 0/7 dimensions support "user inputs idea → user receives product artifact"
    77	
    78	This re-read corrects the 2026-05-14 snapshot §8.3 "Not yet built" + first-cut numbers in this doc's earlier draft, which used a binary "demo works? if not → 0%" cut that obscured the substantial brick-level investment.
    79	
    80	#### Distance to Phase 1 (operator-stated goal: "user generates one complete pure-software website OR mobile app")
    81	
    82	Two paths matter:
    83	
    84	| Goal | Estimated effort | Why this estimate |
    85	|---|---|---|
    86	| **Minimum viable POC** (one skill pack end-to-end, e.g. Next.js): wire `intention-picker` → spec extractor → `nextjs_scaffolder.render_project()` → output a downloadable zip | **1-2 weeks of focused work** | Bricks are ALL there; just needs an orchestrator (`backend/factory/render_orchestrator.py` ~300-500 LOC) + spec extractor + UI wiring. Could ship as `[OP-15xx] Phase 1 POC: idea→Next.js project zip` |
    87	| **Web + Mobile coverage** (5 skill packs: nextjs/nuxt/ios/android/flutter): same POC pattern across all five | +1-2 weeks | Per-skill orchestrator code is ~80% reusable |
    88	| **Deploy target** (user clicks → gets hosted URL on Vercel / self-hosted / etc.) | +2-4 weeks | Depends on choice: Vercel API integration / Cloudflare Pages / self-hosted Caddy; multi-tenant deployment isolation; user-facing rollback |
    89	| **Full Layer 2b user-agent** ([[project_runner_pivot_strategic_reframe]] §7 Phase 7) | **6+ months** | Per the strategic phasing; bootstrap the user-driven agent layer that uses all 7 dimensions |
    90	
    91	**Key insight**: the gap is NOT "do we have building blocks". The gap is **"is anyone wiring them into a user-driven flow"**. Per recent work pattern, ~95% of new tickets target Layer 1 + Layer 2a (infrastructure + dev-runner). Layer 2b user-agent has had **near-zero direct investment** in the last 4 weeks (WP.4 intention picker + WP.10 BP Fleet UI Lanes were the only Layer 2b touches in May 2026, and even those are display-only, not generative).
    92	
    93	#### What the system CAN do today
    94	
    95	- Run an autonomous **dev runner** that ships its own infrastructure improvements via Gerrit (proven via TODO.md 6,851-line completion + 2026-05-20 OP-1524 runner self-repair)
    96	- Maintain its own image pipeline end-to-end (Gerrit → GitLab CR → prod deploy with cosign signing + SBOM + attestation, per [[project_op_1478_gitlab_cr_activation_2026_05_19]])
    97	- Survive host cold-start without operator intervention ([[reference_cold_start_safety_inventory]])
    98	- **Library-level**: programmatically invoke any of 10 scaffolders to generate per-framework skeleton projects (developer-shell access required, not user-driven)
    99	- **Library-level**: mobile App Store / Play Store compliance audits via `backend.mobile_compliance` CLI
   100	- Support 5 concurrent agents via shared Layer 1 primitives
   101	
   102	#### What the system CANNOT do today
   103	
   104	- Take an idea ("I want to make a baby monitor" OR "I want a todo app") and produce a manufactured product OR a deployable web/mobile app **end-to-end through a user-facing flow**
   105	- Generate a real BOM
   106	- Run any user-driven compliance workflow
   107	- Talk to a real supplier or contract manufacturer
   108	- Simulate any physical property
   109	- **Critically**: even though `nextjs_scaffolder` works at library level, there is NO user path from "I open OmniSight UI" → "I describe my todo app" → "I download a working Next.js project" — that path is the missing orchestrator + spec extractor + UI wiring
   110	
   111	#### Strategic tension worth holding
   112	
   113	The 2026-05-14 §7 "Bootstrap Self-Reference" claims "product builds itself" as strategic advantage. Empirical realization 2026-05-20: this currently means **"runner strengthens runner"** (OP-1524 self-repair / OP-1525 filing UX), NOT **"runner produces real product artifacts"**. The 6-month phasing assumption is that strengthened Layer 1 + Layer 2a eventually bootstrap Layer 2b. **This assumption has not yet been validated** at the user-flow level (only at library level).
   114	
   115	The first real test will be when an explicit "Phase 1 POC" attempt happens (estimated 1-2 weeks focused work using existing bricks) — then we'll know empirically if the brick→orchestration→user-flow path actually flows OR if there are hidden integration gaps.
   116	
   117	---

   215	### BP.A2A — Agent-to-Agent Protocol Integration（~2-3 週）— **2026-05-02 新增 (from Agentic Design Patterns Ch 15)**
   216	> 借鑑 [Agentic Design Patterns Ch 15 — Inter-Agent Communication / A2A](https://github.com/evoiz/Agentic-Design-Patterns)。OmniSight 本質就是 multi-agent + multi-provider 編排器，A2A 是這個架構的 native protocol layer。**範圍嚴格分清**：內部 specialist routing 仍走 LangGraph state（不動）；A2A 只負責 cross-process / cross-vendor / cross-system edge —— 跨 vendor 統一介面、外部 agent invocation、對外暴露 OmniSight agent 給 enterprise client / partner / 第三方系統。**前置**：BP.B (Guild 重組) 完成後 capability descriptor 才有清楚對映；建議跟 BP.F 同期或之後做，因為 A2A 是 BP.F 的 agent-level companion (BP.F 抽象 LLM-call 級 provider，A2A 抽象 agent-call 級 provider)。

     1	"""Health & readiness probes.
     2	
     3	Two distinct concerns, two distinct endpoints:
     4	
     5	* ``/healthz`` — **liveness**. Must return 200 fast whenever the Python
     6	  process is alive enough to answer an HTTP request. No I/O. No DB.
     7	  The orchestrator uses this to decide "is the process dead, should I
     8	  restart it?". ``/livez`` is a byte-identical alias — the charter
     9	  (``docs/ops/orchestration_selection.md`` §7.3) commits K8s probes to
    10	  the ``/livez`` spelling, so G5 #4 wires there; ``/healthz`` stays the
    11	  historical canonical path for compose / systemd / CF checks.
    12	
    13	* ``/readyz`` — **readiness**. Returns 200 only when the process is
    14	  ready to serve traffic: DB is reachable, alembic migrations are at
    15	  head, and at least one LLM provider in the fallback chain has
    16	  credentials (or is the Ollama local fallback, which needs none).
    17	  Starts failing immediately once ``_lifecycle.coordinator`` flips to
    18	  draining — this is the mechanism by which the orchestrator takes a
    19	  replica out of rotation during graceful shutdown.
    20	
    21	``/api/v1/health`` is kept as a legacy alias returning the liveness
    22	payload so existing wizard/UI consumers keep working.
    23	"""
    24	
    25	from __future__ import annotations
    26	
    27	import logging
    28	import time
    29	from pathlib import Path
    30	
    31	from fastapi import APIRouter, Request
    32	from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
    33	
    34	from backend.models import HealthResponse
    35	
    36	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37

    39	# Router mounted under ``settings.api_prefix`` — preserves the legacy
    40	# ``/api/v1/health`` contract.
    41	router = APIRouter(tags=["health"])
    42	
    43	# Router mounted at the server root so probes answer on ``/healthz``
    44	# and ``/readyz`` without an API prefix (k8s / systemd / docker-compose
    45	# / Cloudflare health checks expect root-level paths).
    46	probe_router = APIRouter(tags=["health"])
    47	
    48	
    49	_VERSION = "0.1.0"
    50	_PHASE = "3.2"
    51	_ENGINE = "OmniSight Engine"
    52	
    53

    55	async def health_check() -> dict:
    56	    """Legacy liveness payload (kept for backwards compatibility)."""
    57	    return {
    58	        "status": "online",
    59	        "engine": _ENGINE,
    60	        "version": _VERSION,

    68	@probe_router.get("/healthz")
    69	async def healthz() -> dict:
    70	    """Liveness probe — fast, no I/O.
    71	
    72	    Returns 200 whenever the event loop can service the request. We
    73	    intentionally do *not* check the DB here: a DB stall should not
    74	    make the orchestrator restart a healthy process — that is the job
    75	    of ``/readyz``.
    76	    """
    77	    return {"status": "ok", "live": True}

    91	# contract regardless of which spelling it probes.

   104	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   105	async def _check_db() -> tuple[bool, str]:
   106	    """Returns (ok, detail). Detail is a short human-readable string."""
   107	    try:
   108	        from backend.db_pool import get_pool
   109	        async with get_pool().acquire() as conn:
   110	            result = await conn.fetchval("SELECT 1")
   111	        if result != 1:
   112	            return False, "db_ping_no_row"
   113	        return True, "ok"
   114	    except RuntimeError as exc:
   115	        return False, f"db_not_initialized: {exc}"
   116	    except Exception as exc:
   117	        return False, f"db_ping_failed: {exc}"
   118	
   119	
   120	async def _check_migrations() -> tuple[bool, str]:
   121	    """Compare alembic's current head with the migrations DAG heads.
   122	
   123	    We avoid importing SQLAlchemy's heavy env setup — instead we do a
   124	    direct read against the ``alembic_version`` table (populated by
   125	    alembic after each upgrade) and compare it with the DAG head leaves
   126	    on disk.  Returns (ok, detail).  If the alembic table doesn't
   127	    exist yet (fresh install), we treat that as *ready* via the legacy
   128	    schema path — db.init() builds the raw schema for non-alembic dev
   129	    flows.
   130	
   131	    SP-5.9 (2026-04-21): SQLite-vs-PG dialect dispatch removed
   132	    (runtime is PG-only now). Always uses ``information_schema.tables``
   133	    for the "does alembic_version exist?" probe.
   134	
   135	    OP-1447 (2026-05-18): replaced the alphabetical filename-sort
   136	    heuristic with ``alembic.script.ScriptDirectory.get_heads()``. The
   137	    old logic inferred "latest revision" from the lexicographically
   138	    last filename in ``alembic/versions/``, which broke once merge-node
   139	    files named ``m_*.py`` were added — ASCII ``'m' > '0'-'9'`` put
   140	    them after the numeric files, so the probe declared
   141	    ``migration_pending`` even when the DB was at the real numeric
   142	    head (e.g. ``0242``). Querying the DAG directly is order-insensitive.
   143	    """
   144	    try:
   145	        from backend.db_pool import get_pool
   146	        async with get_pool().acquire() as conn:
   147	            table_exists = await conn.fetchval(
   148	                "SELECT EXISTS("
   149	                "  SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.tables "
   150	                "  WHERE table_schema = 'public' "
   151	                "  AND table_name = 'alembic_version'"
   152	                ")"
   153	            )
   154	            if not table_exists:
   155	                return True, "alembic_not_applied_legacy_schema"
   156	            current = await conn.fetchval(
   157	                "SELECT version_num FROM alembic_version"
   158	            )
   159	    except Exception as exc:
   160	        return False, f"alembic_probe_failed: {exc}"
   161	    if not current:
   162	        return False, "alembic_version_empty"
   163	
   164	    script_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "alembic"
   165	    if not script_dir.is_dir():
   166	        return True, f"current={current},no_versions_dir"
   167	
   168	    try:
   169	        from alembic.config import Config
   170	        from alembic.script import ScriptDirectory
   171	
   172	        cfg = Config()
   173	        cfg.set_main_option("script_location", str(script_dir))
   174	        heads = set(ScriptDirectory.from_config(cfg).get_heads())
   175	    except Exception as exc:
   176	        return False, f"alembic_script_dir_unreadable: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
   177	
   178	    if not heads:
   179	        return True, f"current={current},no_migrations"
   180	
   181	    if current not in heads:
   182	        heads_str = "|".join(sorted(heads))
   183	        return False, f"migration_pending: current={current} heads={heads_str}"
   184	
   185	    return True, f"current={current}"
   186	
   187	
   188	#: C3 — deep-check result cache. Maps ``(provider, key_suffix)`` →
   189	#: ``(ok, detail, expires_at)``. Keyed on the key suffix so a rotation
   190	#: invalidates the cache entry on the next probe. 60 s TTL is long
   191	#: enough to keep `/readyz` cheap under frequent probes (Caddy polls
   192	#: every 2 s during a rolling deploy → 30 cache hits per miss) but
   193	#: short enough that a newly-broken provider is caught within a minute.
   194	_DEEP_CHECK_CACHE: dict[tuple[str, str], tuple[bool, str, float]] = {}
   195	_DEEP_CHECK_TTL_S = 60.0
   196	#: Network timeout per provider probe. Shorter than the Caddy probe
   197	#: cycle so a hung provider can't inflate `/readyz` latency past the
   198	#: proxy timeout.
   199	_DEEP_CHECK_TIMEOUT_S = 3.0
   200	
   201	
   202	def _provider_probe_url(provider: str) -> str | None:
   203	    """Cheapest GET per provider. Authenticated list/models endpoints
   204	    that verify the key is *usable*, not just syntactically present.
   205	
   206	    Chosen to be:
   207	      * idempotent (safe to poll);
   208	      * <1 KB response (fast even on flaky links);
   209	      * rejected with 401 on invalid key (so we can distinguish
   210	        "network down" from "key revoked" from "quota exhausted").
   211	    """
   212	    return {
   213	        "anthropic": "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/models",
   214	        "openai": "https://api.openai.com/v1/models",
   215	        "google": "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models",
   216	        "openrouter": "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/models",
   217	        "groq": "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/models",
   218	        "deepseek": "https://api.deepseek.com/v1/models",
   219	        "xai": "https://api.x.ai/v1/models",
   220	        "together": "https://api.together.xyz/v1/models",
   221	    }.get(provider)
   222	
   223	
   224	def _probe_provider_deep(provider: str, key: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   225	    """Make one authenticated GET. Returns ``(ok, detail)``.
   226	
   227	    Cached in ``_DEEP_CHECK_CACHE`` on the ``(provider, key_tail)``
   228	    key so rotation invalidates naturally. All network errors are
   229	    treated as ``not_ok`` — if the provider is unreachable, we're
   230	    not ready to serve requests that depend on it.
   231	    """
   232	    import time as _time
   233	    import urllib.request
   234	    import urllib.error
   235	
   236	    # key_tail = last 6 chars, opaque to logs but unique across rotations
   237	    key_tail = key[-6:] if key else ""
   238	    cache_key = (provider, key_tail)
   239	    cached = _DEEP_CHECK_CACHE.get(cache_key)
   240	    now = _time.time()
   241	    if cached and cached[2] > now:
   242	        return cached[0], cached[1] + ":cached"
   243	
   244	    url = _provider_probe_url(provider)
   245	    if url is None:
   246	        # Unknown provider — behave like a presence check did before.
   247	        ok = bool(key)
   248	        detail = "key_present" if ok else "no_key"
   249	        _DEEP_CHECK_CACHE[cache_key] = (ok, detail, now + _DEEP_CHECK_TTL_S)
   250	        return ok, detail
   251	
   252	    # Provider-specific auth header. Anthropic and Google use
   253	    # non-standard headers; everyone else uses Bearer.
   254	    if provider == "anthropic":
   255	        headers = {"x-api-key": key, "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01"}
   256	    elif provider == "google":
   257	        # Google's API key rides in the query string, not a header.
   258	        url = f"{url}?key={key}"
   259	        headers = {}
   260	    else:

   222	
   223	## 4. Idempotence contract (AC #4)
   224	
   225	A `release_id` is the deduplication key for the whole pipeline. The
   226	orchestrator guarantees:
   227	
   228	1. A duplicate POST with `release_id = X` whose ledger row is
   229	   `status='completed'` returns HTTP 200 with
   230	   `idempotent_replay=true` and runs **zero** side effects (no
   231	   docker pull, no SSE re-emit, no audit chain entry).
   232	2. A duplicate POST whose ledger row is `status='running'` returns
   233	   HTTP 409 (`ConcurrentDeployInFlight`). The race-loser's POST
   234	   never publishes `prod.deploy.started`.
   235	3. A duplicate POST whose row is `aborted_*` is treated as a fresh
   236	   attempt **only if you supply a new `release_id`**. Reusing an
   237	   `aborted_*` release_id will fail the UNIQUE constraint.
   238	
   239	Rationale for §3: an `aborted_*` outcome means we have not validated
   240	the prerequisites, so we don't want a quick retry under the same id
   241	to skip the smoke step.
   242	
   243	---
   244	
   245	## 5. SSE event contract (AC #5)
   246	
   247	Three events on the global SSE bus
   248	(`prod.deploy.{started,completed,aborted}`):
   249	
   250	```json
   251	// prod.deploy.started
   252	{"release_id": "...", "image_tag": "...", "actor": "...", "timestamp": "..."}
   253	
   254	// prod.deploy.completed
   255	{"release_id": "...", "image_tag": "...", "elapsed_seconds": 412.7, "timestamp": "..."}
   256	
   257	// prod.deploy.aborted
   258	{
   259	  "release_id": "...",
   260	  "image_tag": "...",
   261	  "status": "aborted_smoke_failed",     // one of the aborted_* terminal states
   262	  "error_class": "PreCheckSmokeFailed", // matches the §AC error catalog
   263	  "error_message": "staging-mirror smoke failed: auth_smoke,checkout_smoke",
   264	  "timestamp": "..."
   265	}
   266	```
   267	
   268	The frontend release dashboard (OP-771 / D17) subscribes via SSE and
   269	updates the deploy lane in real time. The events are NOT replayed by
   270	an idempotent re-run — that's deliberate, so the dashboard's
   271	"deploys today" counter stays accurate.
   272	
   273	---

     4	; in release_milestone_checker (it only requires the latest record for the

     1	; [OP-965] AUDIT-17 — staging gate "smoke" status emitter.

     5	; scripts/release_milestone_checker.py reads for the R3 ``smoke_suite``
     6	; gate. The checker requires that record be < 4h old, so the matching
     7	; timer fires every 30 min (see staging-gate-smoke.timer) — comfortably
     8	; inside the window even after a missed tick.

Product docs are more honest and say end-to-end user flows are not built: [docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md:62), [docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md:76), [docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md:104).

**Effort**

Reusable pieces exist: `dag_schema`, `dag_validator`, `dag_storage` state machine, `workflow.step`, `t3_resolver`, `container.start_container/dispatch_t3`, `ssh_runner.run_on_target`, `sandbox_prewarm`, platform profiles/toolchain metadata.

Missing pieces: durable executor loop; multi-replica claim/lease for `dag_plans`; topological ready-task scheduler; task-type-to-handler dispatch for `compile`, `flash`, `cross-compile`, `package`; artifact/output validation; per-task step recording tied to `workflow_steps.dag_task_id`; `dag_plans.completed` + `workflow.finish()` wiring; failure semantics; side-effect isolation for staging; idempotent resume after crash.

Smallest viable slice for `smoke-compile-flash-host-native`: 3-5 person-days, medium risk. Implement a single-process executor that claims one `executing` plan, rehydrates the DAG, runs only host-native test-mode handlers for `compile` and symbolic `flash`, records two `workflow.step` rows, sets `dag_plans.completed`, then calls `workflow.finish(run_id, "completed")`. Multi-replica-safe production execution with remote SSH/cross-compile/package and artifact isolation is more like 8-15 person-days, higher risk.

Final: **claim TRUE**, blast-radius **High** because release smoke is a promotion gate and cannot honestly pass, effort **3-5 days for smoke-only MVP / 8-15 days for production-grade DAG executor**.

- `/readyz` is not a DAG completion check; it checks process readiness, DB, migrations, and provider credentials: [backend/routers/health.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/routers/health.py:13), [backend/routers/health.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/routers/health.py:105), [backend/routers/health.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/routers/health.py:120).
- Bootstrap Step 5 is not broken in the same way because it was rewritten to accept validate+persist only. The comments explicitly say no gcc/cmake/cross-toolchain runs, and success is `plan_status in ("validated", "executing")`: [backend/routers/bootstrap.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/routers/bootstrap.py:2593), [backend/routers/bootstrap.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/routers/bootstrap.py:2601), [backend/routers/bootstrap.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/routers/bootstrap.py:2905).
- Misleading surfaces remain: TODO still labels A2 as “2 real DAGs end-to-end” while noting the observed run had `status=running` and `step_count=0`: [TODO.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/TODO.md:131), [TODO.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/TODO.md:135). Product docs are more honest and say end-to-end user flows are not built: [docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md:62), [docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md:76), [docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/product/2026-05-20-what-is-omnisight.md:104).

**Effort**

Reusable pieces exist: `dag_schema`, `dag_validator`, `dag_storage` state machine, `workflow.step`, `t3_resolver`, `container.start_container/dispatch_t3`, `ssh_runner.run_on_target`, `sandbox_prewarm`, platform profiles/toolchain metadata.

Missing pieces: durable executor loop; multi-replica claim/lease for `dag_plans`; topological ready-task scheduler; task-type-to-handler dispatch for `compile`, `flash`, `cross-compile`, `package`; artifact/output validation; per-task step recording tied to `workflow_steps.dag_task_id`; `dag_plans.completed` + `workflow.finish()` wiring; failure semantics; side-effect isolation for staging; idempotent resume after crash.

Smallest viable slice for `smoke-compile-flash-host-native`: 3-5 person-days, medium risk. Implement a single-process executor that claims one `executing` plan, rehydrates the DAG, runs only host-native test-mode handlers for `compile` and symbolic `flash`, records two `workflow.step` rows, sets `dag_plans.completed`, then calls `workflow.finish(run_id, "completed")`. Multi-replica-safe production execution with remote SSH/cross-compile/package and artifact isolation is more like 8-15 person-days, higher risk.

Final: **claim TRUE**, blast-radius **High** because release smoke is a promotion gate and cannot honestly pass, effort **3-5 days for smoke-only MVP / 8-15 days for production-grade DAG executor**.
